### [1] [Let Functions Speak: Lightweight Parametric Polymorphism via Domain and Range Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23360)
*Siyuan He,Songlin Jia,Tiark Rompf*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: F<:DR 演算引入域和范围投影类型 Dom(T) 和 Range(T)，用于类型化任意类型 T 的函数应用，解决了 TypeScript 中依赖 unsafe `any` 类型的装饰器模式类型安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TypeScript 中常见的函数装饰器模式使用 Parameters<T> 和 ReturnType<T> 类型，但实践中依赖 unsafe 的 `any` 类型，削弱了静态类型保证。核心问题在于标准类型原则：函数应用仅在被调用者被暴露为箭头类型时才合理。

Method: 提出 F<:DR 演算，添加域和范围投影类型 Dom(T) 和 Range(T) 用于任意类型 T。这些投影允许通过抽象函数类型进行类型化应用：Dom(T) 类型的参数见证可调用性，结果被类型化为 Range(T)。该设计补充而非替代基于箭头的标准应用，后者通过 System F<: 中的子类型保持可接受性。

Result: 在 Rocq 中实现了 F<:DR 演算，并使用带路径选择的逻辑关系证明了语义类型安全性，该技术延迟投影解释直到函数结构被解析。相同技术可扩展到其他投影类型，如原始对（乘积类型）。

Conclusion: F<:DR 演算通过引入域和范围投影类型，为动态语言中的函数装饰器模式提供了更安全的类型系统，解决了 TypeScript 中依赖 unsafe `any` 的问题，同时保持了与标准箭头类型应用的兼容性。

Abstract: Dynamic languages such as Python and JavaScript widely use function decorators to extend behavior. In TypeScript, a common way to type such patterns uses Parameters<T> and ReturnType<T>. In practice, this idiom relies on a function-type bound for T that is expressed using the unsafe type any, which weakens static guarantees. At the core is a standard typing principle: application is justified only when the callee is exposed as an arrow type.
  We present F<:DR, a calculus that adds domain and range projection types, Dom(T) and Range(T), for arbitrary types T. These projections permit typing applications through abstract function types: an argument of type Dom(T) witnesses callability, and the result is typed as Range(T). This design complements, rather than replaces, standard arrow-based application, which remains admissible via subtyping in System F<:.
  We mechanize F<:DR in Rocq and prove semantic type soundness using logical relations with path selection, which delays projection interpretation until function structure is resolved. The same technique extends to additional projection types, illustrated for primitive pairs, i.e., product types.

</details>

### [2] [Working towards a dialectical understanding of the political ideology within technological projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22436)
*Frederick Reiber*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个理解技术项目政治意识形态的辩证框架，强调项目的价值观和约束条件对于理解其意识形态都是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在开发一个理解技术项目政治意识形态的辩证框架，基于批判性和解放性社会科学讨论，强调需要同时考虑项目的价值观和约束条件来全面理解其意识形态。

Method: 采用批判性和解放性社会科学理论，构建一个辩证框架，强调价值观和约束条件的相互作用，并通过一个简要示例来帮助理解。

Result: 提出了一个理解技术项目政治意识形态的辩证框架，该框架能够更全面地分析技术项目的意识形态维度。

Conclusion: 理解技术项目的政治意识形态需要同时考虑其价值观和约束条件，这个辩证框架为分析技术项目的意识形态提供了更全面的视角。

Abstract: In this short position paper, I develop a dialectical framework for understanding the political ideology of technological projects. To do so, I draw on critical and emancipatory social science discussions, highlighting how both a project's values and constraints are necessary for understanding its ideology. A brief example is then presented to aid comprehension.

</details>


### [3] ["Chasing Shadows": Understanding Personal Data Externalization and Self-Tracking for Neurodivergent Individuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22609)
*Tanya Rudberg Selin,Danielle Unéus,Søren Knudsen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 神经多样性个体在追踪"伪装"行为时的体验研究：发现自我追踪带来解释和情感负担，需要结合同伴支持和情境考量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于神经多样性个体（自闭症和/或ADHD）在自我追踪"伪装"行为时的体验与主流自我追踪理论（强调自我洞察）之间的脱节。作为神经多样性研究者，作者发现自我追踪很少能带来预期的自我洞察，因此希望深入理解这种脱节的原因。

Method: 采用两阶段定性研究方法：第一阶段工作坊，6名自闭症和/或ADHD参与者制作伪装体验的视觉表征；第二阶段，3名参与者设计并使用个性化的自我追踪工具，聚焦"去伪装"过程，持续两周。使用反思性主题分析法分析活动记录和访谈数据。

Result: 研究发现：1）自我追踪带来巨大的解释和情感需求，情境依赖性挑战了自我追踪的假设；2）促进经验分享可以验证情感反应并支持反思；3）识别出影响个人数据参与的三个情感维度，构建了自我追踪中情感的工作模型。

Conclusion: 结论指出需要重新设计自我追踪和反思实践，纳入同伴支持，更好地考虑情境因素和情感劳动。研究为神经多样性个体的自我追踪提供了新的设计方向，强调情感维度和情境敏感性的重要性。

Abstract: We examine how neurodivergent individuals experience creating, interacting with, and reflecting on personal data about masking. Although self-tracking is often framed as enabling self-insight, this is rarely our experience as neurodivergent individuals and researchers. To better understand this disconnect, we conducted a two-phase qualitative study. First, a workshop where six participants with autism and/or ADHD crafted visual representations of masking experiences. Then, three participants continued by designing and using personalized self-tracking focused on unmasking over two weeks. Using reflexive thematic analysis of activities and interviews, we find that self-tracking imposes substantial interpretive and emotional demands, shaped by context-dependencies that challenge assumptions in self-tracking. We also find that facilitated sharing of experiences might validate emotional responses and support reflection. We identify three emotional dimensions that shape engagement with personal data in a working model of emotion in self-tracking, and discuss implications for designing self-tracking and reflective practices that incorporate peer support and better account for context and emotional labor.

</details>


### [4] [Learning to Trust: How Humans Mentally Recalibrate AI Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22634)
*ZhaoBin Li,Mark Steyvers*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 人类能够通过经验学习来心理校准AI置信度信号，但存在边界：对于单调校准偏差可以补偿，但对于反直觉的"反向置信度"映射则难以克服初始归纳偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人类是否能够通过重复经验学习来心理校准AI的置信度信号，以解决AI系统常见的校准不当问题（系统性过度自信或自信不足），从而促进更有效的人机协作。

Method: 行为实验（N=200）：参与者预测AI正确性，测试四种AI校准条件（标准、过度自信、自信不足、反直觉的"反向置信度"映射）。计算模型采用线性对数几率（LLO）变换和Rescorla-Wagner学习规则来解释学习动态。

Result: 在所有条件下，参与者经过50次试验后显著提高了准确性、辨别力和校准对齐度。计算模型显示人类通过更新基线信任和置信度敏感性来适应，使用不对称学习率优先处理信息量最大的错误。人类能够补偿单调校准偏差，但在反向置信度场景中存在显著边界，相当比例的参与者难以克服初始归纳偏差。

Conclusion: 人类能够通过经验适应对AI置信度信号的信任，但对于反直觉的校准模式存在学习边界。这为理解人类如何通过经验调整对AI置信度的信任提供了机制性解释。

Abstract: Productive human-AI collaboration requires appropriate reliance, yet contemporary AI systems are often miscalibrated, exhibiting systematic overconfidence or underconfidence. We investigate whether humans can learn to mentally recalibrate AI confidence signals through repeated experience. In a behavioral experiment (N = 200), participants predicted the AI's correctness across four AI calibration conditions: standard, overconfidence, underconfidence, and a counterintuitive "reverse confidence" mapping. Results demonstrate robust learning across all conditions, with participants significantly improving their accuracy, discrimination, and calibration alignment over 50 trials. We present a computational model utilizing a linear-in-log-odds (LLO) transformation and a Rescorla-Wagner learning rule to explain these dynamics. The model reveals that humans adapt by updating their baseline trust and confidence sensitivity, using asymmetric learning rates to prioritize the most informative errors. While humans can compensate for monotonic miscalibration, we identify a significant boundary in the reverse confidence scenario, where a substantial proportion of participants struggled to override initial inductive biases. These findings provide a mechanistic account of how humans adapt their trust in AI confidence signals through experience.

</details>


### [5] [AwesomeLit: Towards Hypothesis Generation with Agent-Supported Literature Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22648)
*Zefei Xie,Yuhan Guo,Kai Xu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AwesomeLit是一个人类-智能体协作的可视化系统，帮助经验不足的研究者探索不熟悉的研究主题、识别文献空白并生成可行假设，通过透明可控的智能体工作流程和可视化探索路径来解决LLM黑盒和幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对经验不足的研究者在文献研究中面临的挑战：识别现有文献空白和生成可行假设困难；现有通用"深度研究"工具不适用于此特定场景；大型语言模型的"黑盒"特性和幻觉问题导致不信任。

Method: 开发了AwesomeLit系统，具有三个创新特征：1) 透明且用户可操控的智能体工作流程；2) 动态生成的查询探索树，可视化探索路径和来源；3) 语义相似性视图，展示论文间的关系。系统支持用户从一般意图过渡到详细研究主题。

Result: 定性研究显示，AwesomeLit能有效帮助用户探索不熟悉的主题、识别有前景的研究方向，并提高对研究结果的信心。

Conclusion: AwesomeLit通过人类-智能体协作的可视化方法，解决了经验不足研究者在文献研究中的核心挑战，提供了透明、可控且有效的工具来支持研究探索和假设生成。

Abstract: There are different goals for literature research, from understanding an unfamiliar topic to generate hypothesis for the next research project. The nature of literature research also varies according to user's familiarity level of the topic. For inexperienced researchers, identifying gaps in the existing literature and generating feasible hypothesis are crucial but challenging. While general ``deep research'' tools can be used, they are not designed for such use case, thus often not effective. In addition, the ``black box" nature and hallucination of Large Language Models (LLMs) often lead to distrust. In this paper, we introduce a human-agent collaborative visualization system AwesomeLit to address this need. It has several novel features: a transparent user-steerable agentic workflow; a dynamically generated query exploring tree, visualizing the exploration path and provenance; and a semantic similarity view, depicting the relationships between papers. It enables users to transition from general intentions to detailed research topics. Finally, a qualitative study involving several early researchers showed that AwesomeLit is effective in helping users explore unfamiliar topics, identify promising research directions, and improve confidence in research results.

</details>


### [6] [Three Years with Classroom AI in Introductory Programming: Shifts in Student Awareness, Interaction, and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22672)
*Boxuan Ma,Huiyong Li,Gen Li,Li Chen,Cheng Tang,Atsushi Shimada,Shin'ichi Konomi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究追踪三个连续AI支持编程课程的学生群体，分析学生与生成式AI关系如何随时间系统性演变，发现AI使用日益常态化，帮助寻求模式随AI素养提升而进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究主要关注AI在计算教育的即时影响，但缺乏纵向证据了解学生AI意识、交互模式和课程成果如何随着AI在课堂中常规化而演变。需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 研究三个连续AI支持的Python入门课程队列（2023-2025），使用问卷调查、编码的学生-AI对话日志和课程评估记录，分析队列间的变化。

Result: 学生与生成式AI的关系随时间系统性变化：熟悉度和使用率日益常态化，帮助寻求实践随着AI素养提升和对助手期望的转变而进化。AI时代教学核心挑战从"是否使用AI"转向"课程如何重新定义富有成效的学习实践同时保持学生自主性"。

Conclusion: 研究提供了纵向证据和实践启示，用于在课程环境中设计和整合AI编程支持，强调需要重新定义学习实践以应对AI常规化带来的挑战。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) tools such as ChatGPT now provide novice programmers with instant, personalized support and are reshaping computing education. While a growing body of work examines AI's immediate impacts, longitudinal evidence remains limited on how students' awareness, student-AI interaction patterns, and course outcomes evolve as AI becomes routine in classrooms. To address this gap, we investigate an introductory Python course across three successive AI-supported cohorts (2023-2025). Using questionnaires, coded student-AI dialogue logs, and course assessment records, we examine cohort-to-cohort shifts in students' AI awareness, interaction practices, and learning outcomes. We find that students' relationships with GenAI change systematically over time: familiarity and uptake become increasingly normative, and help-seeking practices evolve alongside growing AI literacy and shifting expectations of what the assistant should provide. These changes suggest that, in the AI era, the central instructional challenge is less about whether students use AI and more about how courses redefine productive learning practices while maintaining student agency. Our study offers longitudinal evidence and practical implications for designing and integrating AI programming support in course settings.

</details>


### [7] [Design Implications for Student and Educator Needs in AI-Supported Programming Learning Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22673)
*Boxuan Ma,Yinjie Xie,Huiyong Li,Gen Li,Li Chen,Atsushi Shimada,Shin'Ichi Konomi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过调查教育者（N=50）和学生（N=90）的偏好，为面向学习的AI编程助手提供设计建议，发现教育者偏好间接脚手架支持，学生偏好直接帮助，需要平衡学生自主性和教学约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注单个AI编程助手工具，缺乏基于教育者和学生双方视角的证据性设计建议，需要为面向学习的AI编程助手提供基于双方需求的设计基础。

Method: 采用并行调查方法，分别调查教育者（50人）和学生（90人），比较双方在三个方面的偏好：学生如何请求帮助、AI如何响应、以及谁应该控制帮助过程。

Result: 教育者普遍倾向于间接脚手架支持以保护学生推理能力，而学生更偏好直接、可操作的帮助。教育者强调需要课程对齐的约束和面向教师的监督功能，学生则重视及时支持和遇到困难时的清晰指导。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，讨论了交互为中心的设计空间，为面向学习的AI编程助手提出设计建议，强调需要平衡学生自主性和教学约束的脚手架和控制机制。

Abstract: AI-powered coding assistants can support students in programming courses by providing on-demand explanations and debugging help. However, existing research often focuses on individual tools, leaving a gap in evidence-based design recommendations that reflect both educator and student perspectives in education settings. To ground the design of learning-oriented AI coding assistants for both sides' needs, we conducted parallel surveys of educators (N=50) and students (N=90) to compare preferences about (i) how students should request help, (ii) how AI should respond, and (iii) who should control. Our results show that educators generally favored indirect scaffolding that preserves students' reasoning, whereas students were more likely to prefer direct, actionable help. Educators further highlighted the need for course-aligned constraints and instructor-facing oversight, while students emphasized timely support and clarity when stuck. Based on these findings, we discuss the interaction-focused design space and derive design implications for learning-oriented AI coding assistants, highlighting scaffolding and control mechanisms that balance students' agency with instructional constraints.

</details>


### [8] [Designing a Meta-Reflective Dashboard for Instructor Insight into Student-AI Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22674)
*Boxuan Ma,Baofeng Ren,Huiyong Li,Gen Li,Li Chen,Atsushi Shimada,Shin'Ichi Konomi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出元反思仪表板，通过AI生成结构化会话摘要，使教师能了解学生与AI的互动情况，避免直接访问原始聊天记录带来的隐私和可扩展性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具被广泛用于课程作业辅助，但学生与AI的对话对教师不可见，导致教师难以了解学生困难、确保课程目标一致性和执行课程政策。直接访问原始聊天记录存在可扩展性、隐私和伦理问题。

Method: 提出元反思仪表板，在每个帮助寻求会话后，反思AI生成结构化会话级摘要，包括学生互动轨迹、AI使用模式和潜在风险。与教师和学生共同设计仪表板，并进行形成性评估，考察感知有用性、摘要可信度和隐私可接受性。

Result: 研究发现，提出的仪表板可以减少教师的感知努力，同时减轻与原始聊天记录访问相关的隐私担忧。研究还产生了关于证据、治理和可扩展的班级级AI支持学习分析的设计启示。

Conclusion: 元反思仪表板为解决教师需要可操作洞察与避免默认监控学生对话之间的紧张关系提供了一种可行方案，通过结构化摘要平衡了教学监督需求与隐私保护。

Abstract: Generative AI tools are increasingly used for coursework help, shifting much of students' help-seeking and reasoning into student-AI chats that are largely invisible to instructors. This loss of visibility can weaken instructors' ability to understand students' difficulties, ensure alignment with course goals, and uphold course policies. Yet transcript-level access is neither scalable nor ethically straightforward: reading raw chat logs across a class is impractical, and exposing detailed dialogue can raise privacy concerns and chilling effects on help seeking. As a result, instructors face a tension between needing actionable insight and avoiding default surveillance of student conversations. To address this gap, we propose a meta-reflective dashboard that makes student-AI sessions interpretable without exposing raw chat logs by default. After each help-seeking session, a reflection AI produces a structured, session-level summary of the student's interaction trajectory, AI usage patterns, and potential risks. We co-designed the dashboard with instructors and students to surface key challenges and design goals, and conducted a formative evaluation of perceived usefulness, trust in the summaries, and privacy acceptability. Findings suggest that the proposed dashboard can reduce instructors' sensemaking effort while mitigating privacy concerns associated with transcript-level access, and they also yield design implications for evidence, governance, and scalable class-level analytics for AI-supported learning.

</details>


### [9] [From Overload to Convergence: Supporting Multi-Issue Human-AI Negotiation with Bayesian Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22766)
*Mehul Parmar,Chaklam Silpasuwanchai*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索了AI中介谈判中议题数量对人类表现的影响，发现无支持时人类最多能处理3个议题，超过后表现下降，并提出基于贝叶斯估计的不确定性可视化方法改善谈判效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地中介谈判，理解谈判议题数量如何影响人类表现对于保持人类能动性至关重要。需要研究人类在复杂AI中介谈判中的认知限制，并开发支持工具来维持人类控制权。

Method: 设计了现实房产租赁场景中的人-AI谈判案例研究，通过变化谈判议题数量进行实验。引入基于贝叶斯估计协议概率的新型不确定性可视化方法，展示相互可接受协议空间随谈判进展而缩小的过程。采用被试内实验设计（N=32）评估可视化效果。

Result: 无支持情况下，人类表现在前三个议题保持稳定，但随着额外议题增加导致认知负荷增加而下降。不确定性可视化显著改善了人类谈判结果和效率，同时保持了人类控制权，避免了价值重新分配。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了人类在人-AI谈判中可管理复杂性的实际限制，推进了复杂谈判中人类表现的理论理解，并为交互系统提供了经过验证的设计指导。不确定性可视化是有效的支持工具，能在保持人类控制的同时改善谈判效果。

Abstract: As AI systems increasingly mediate negotiations, understanding how the number of negotiated issues impacts human performance is crucial for maintaining human agency. We designed a human-AI negotiation case study in a realistic property rental scenario, varying the number of negotiated issues; empirical findings show that without support, performance stays stable up to three issues but declines as additional issues increase cognitive load. To address this, we introduce a novel uncertainty-based visualization driven by Bayesian estimation of agreement probability. It shows how the space of mutually acceptable agreements narrows as negotiation progresses, helping users identify promising options. In a within-subjects experiment (N=32), it improved human outcomes and efficiency, preserved human control, and avoided redistributing value. Our findings surface practical limits on the complexity people can manage in human-AI negotiation, advance theory on human performance in complex negotiations, and offer validated design guidance for interactive systems.

</details>


### [10] [DiSCo: Diffusion Sequence Copilots for Shared Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22787)
*Andy Wang,Xu Yan,Brandon McMahan,Michael Zhou,Yuyang Yuan,Johannes Y. Lee,Ali Shreif,Matthew Li,Zhenghao Peng,Bolei Zhou,Yuchen Cui,Jonathan C. Kao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DiSCo是一种基于扩散策略的共享自主方法，通过扩散过程规划与用户历史动作一致的动作序列，在模拟驾驶和机械臂任务中显著提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当任务具有挑战性、需要高维控制或受到干扰时，共享自主性可以通过训练好的副驾驶有效纠正用户动作来显著提高任务性能。现有方法需要进一步改进共享自主性的性能。

Method: 提出扩散序列副驾驶(DiSCo)：一种基于扩散策略的共享自主方法，通过扩散过程规划与过去用户动作一致的动作序列。方法通过种子化和修复扩散过程，使用用户提供的动作和超参数来平衡专家动作的符合性、用户意图的对齐以及感知响应性。

Result: DiSCo在模拟驾驶和机械臂任务中显著提高了任务性能。项目网站提供了详细演示。

Conclusion: DiSCo通过扩散策略有效结合人类用户和AI副驾驶的动作，在复杂系统控制中实现了显著的性能提升，为共享自主性提供了新的有效方法。

Abstract: Shared autonomy combines human user and AI copilot actions to control complex systems such as robotic arms. When a task is challenging, requires high dimensional control, or is subject to corruption, shared autonomy can significantly increase task performance by using a trained copilot to effectively correct user actions in a manner consistent with the user's goals. To significantly improve the performance of shared autonomy, we introduce Diffusion Sequence Copilots (DiSCo): a method of shared autonomy with diffusion policy that plans action sequences consistent with past user actions. DiSCo seeds and inpaints the diffusion process with user-provided actions with hyperparameters to balance conformity to expert actions, alignment with user intent, and perceived responsiveness. We demonstrate that DiSCo substantially improves task performance in simulated driving and robotic arm tasks. Project website: https://sites.google.com/view/disco-shared-autonomy/

</details>


### [11] ["Don't Mess Up My Algorithm": Phatic Communication and Algorithmic Contagion in Meme Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22817)
*Ji Eun Song,Hyunsoo Jang,Juhee Im,Joongseek Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 用户通过私信交换表情包作为社交互动，但认为这会塑造个性化推荐，导致关系实践与算法控制间的张力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究用户在Instagram上如何感知私信表情包交换对推荐算法的影响，以及这种感知如何影响他们的应对策略和无助感，而非审计Instagram的实际推荐机制

Method: 对21名活跃的表情包私信用户进行半结构化访谈，分析用户如何基于关系契合度将表情包分类为"接收者友好"或"接收者不友好"

Result: 用户将不友好表情包的传播视为"算法传染"；控制行为受关系规范、反馈工具感知效能低、私信-推荐关联不透明等因素制约；揭示了基于私信的关系实践如何与个性化基础设施纠缠

Conclusion: 提出三个设计启示：透明的关联解释、对话级别的选择退出机制、以及保守学习（降低私信来源信号的权重）

Abstract: On algorithmic social platforms, exchanging memes via direct messages (DMs) serves as phatic communication that affirms relationships, yet users often interpret these exchanges as signals shaping personalized recommendations, creating tension between relational practice and algorithmic control. This study examines how users perceive DM meme exchanges on Instagram rather than auditing Instagram's underlying recommender mechanisms, and how beliefs about DM-recommendation linkages shape coping strategies and feelings of powerlessness. We conducted semi-structured interviews with 21 active meme-DM users. Participants classified memes as recipient-friendly or recipient-unfriendly based on relational fit; many described the spread of unfriendly memes as "algorithmic contagion." Controls were constrained by relational norms, low perceived efficacy of feedback tools, and opaque DM-recommendation linkages. We articulate how DM-based relational practices are entangled with personalization infrastructures and propose three design implications: transparent linkage explanations, conversation-level opt-outs, and conservative learning that down-weights DM-originated signals.

</details>


### [12] ["Don't Look, But I Know You Do": Norms and Observer Effects in Shared LLM Accounts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22822)
*Ji Eun Song,Eunchae Lee,Juhee Im,Hyunsoo Jang,Eunji Kim,Joongseek Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨生成式AI平台中的账户共享现象，分析共享类型、规范形成及隐私问题，提出从单用户设计转向多用户现实的设计建议


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 账户共享在订阅服务中很常见，现在扩展到生成式AI平台，但这些平台主要设计为单用户使用。共享通常需要变通方法，这会产生新的紧张关系。研究旨在理解LLM订阅如何被共享以及形成的规范。

Method: 结合245名用户的调查和36名参与者的访谈，了解共享模式和实际体验。分析识别了四种账户共享类型，基于两个维度：所有者是否使用账户和订阅成本是否共享。

Result: 识别了四种账户共享类型，考察了规范如何形成及其脆弱性（特别是隐私问题）在实践中如何显现。用户意识到这些问题后微妙调整行为，这可通过观察者效应解释。将LLM账户共享视为一种社会化的挪用实践。

Conclusion: 将LLM账户共享视为一种社会化的挪用实践，提出设计启示，使单用户平台适应多用户现实。

Abstract: Account sharing is common in subscription services and is now extending to generative AI platforms, which are still primarily designed for individual use. Sharing often requires workarounds that create new tensions. This study examines how LLM subscriptions are shared and the norms that develop. We combined a survey of 245 users with interviews of 36 participants to understand both patterns and lived experiences. Our analysis identified four types of account sharing, organized along two dimensions: whether the owner uses the account and whether subscription costs are shared. Within these types, we examined how norms were formed and how their fragility, especially privacy, became evident in practice. Users, fully aware of this, subtly adjusted their behavior, which we interpret through the lens of the observer effect. We frame LLM account sharing as a social practice of appropriation and outline design implications to adapt single-user platforms to multi-user realities.

</details>


### [13] [TastePrint: A 3D Food Printing System for Layer-wise Taste Distribution via Airbrushed Liquid Seasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22887)
*Yamato Miyatake,Parinya Punpongsanon*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: TastePrint：一种3D食品打印系统，通过可编程喷枪动态施加液体调味料，实现层间空间味觉分布，将食品几何形状与味觉设计解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D食品打印虽然能定制食品形状和质地，但由于可打印材料多样性有限，通常产生均匀的味觉分布。需要一种能够实现空间味觉分布的系统，使食品几何形状与味觉设计分离。

Method: 开发了TastePrint系统，包括：(1)图形用户界面，允许用户导入3D模型、切片分层，并为每层指定喷雾位置和强度；(2)定制的3D食品打印机，配备多喷嘴喷雾机制。通过技术实验评估喷雾分辨率和沉积精度，并进行探索性可用性研究。

Result: 喷雾分辨率模型R²=0.86，喷雾量模型R²=0.99。参与者在约15分钟内完成设计任务。系统能够以良好重复性控制调味料位置和数量，支持探索性味觉设计工作流程。

Conclusion: TastePrint为将食品几何形状与味觉设计解耦建立了技术基础，并激励未来对个性化多感官食品制造的感官研究。

Abstract: 3D food printing enables the customization of food shapes and textures, but typically produces uniform taste profiles due to the limited diversity of printable materials. We present TastePrint, a 3D food printing system that achieves layer-wise spatial taste distribution by dynamically applying liquid seasonings with a programmable airbrush during fabrication. The system integrates (1) a graphical user interface (GUI) that allows users to import 3D models, slice them into layers, and specify spray positions and intensities for each layer, and (2) a customized 3D food printer equipped with a multi-nozzle spray mechanism. We evaluated the system through technical experiments quantifying spray resolution and deposition accuracy, together with an exploratory usability study involving three home cooks designing personalized taste patterns. The spray-resolution model achieved R2 = 0.86, the spray-amount model achieved R2 = 0.99, and participants completed the design task in approximately 15 min on average. These results indicate that TastePrint can control seasoning placement and quantity with good repeatability while supporting exploratory taste-design workflows. This work establishes a technical foundation for decoupling food geometry from taste design and motivates future sensory studies on personalized, multisensory food fabrication.

</details>


### [14] [From Morality Installation in LLMs to LLMs in Morality-as-a-System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22944)
*Gunter Bombaerts*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出"道德即系统"框架，将LLM道德视为社会技术系统的动态涌现属性，而非训练时固定的特性，通过七个结构耦合组件持续再生产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM道德研究缺乏连接内部道德表征与监管义务的工具、设计全开发栈文化多元性的方法，以及监控道德属性在系统生命周期中漂移的能力。这些困难源于将道德视为训练时安装的静态属性这一共同根源。

Method: 基于尼克拉斯·卢曼的社会系统理论，提出概念性框架而非实证研究。将LLM道德视为七个结构耦合组件（神经基质、训练数据、对齐程序、系统提示、审核、运行时动态、用户界面）通过交互持续再生产的动态涌现属性。

Result: 从哲学角度重构了三个已知挑战：可解释性-治理鸿沟、跨组件多元性问题、生命周期监控缺失，将其视为安装范式无法诊断的结构耦合失败。为技术研究者提出三个假设，为哲学家和治理专家提供在现有治理框架内指定基质级监控义务的词汇表。

Conclusion: 道德即系统框架不取代宪法AI或RLHF等方法，而是将其嵌入更大的时间和结构解释中，并指定这些方法所需的基础设施。该框架将道德视为系统的动态涌现属性，而非训练时安装的静态特性。

Abstract: Work on morality in large language models (LLMs) has progressed via constitutional AI, reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) and systematic benchmarking, yet it still lacks tools to connect internal moral representations to regulatory obligations, to design cultural plurality across the full development stack, and to monitor how moral properties drift over the lifecycle of a deployed system. These difficulties reflect a shared root. Morality is installed in a model at training time. I propose instead a morality-as-a-system framework, grounded in Niklas Luhmann's social systems theory, that treats LLM morality as a dynamic, emergent property of a sociotechnical system. Moral behaviour in a deployed LLM is not fixed at training. It is continuously reproduced through interactions among seven structurally coupled components spanning the neural substrate, training data, alignment procedures, system prompts, moderation, runtime dynamics, and user interface. This is a conceptual framework paper, not an empirical study. It philosophically reframes three known challenges, the interpretability-governance gap, the cross-component plurality problem, and the absence of lifecycle monitoring, as structural coupling failures that the installation paradigm cannot diagnose. For technical researchers, it explores three illustrative hypotheses about cross-component representational inconsistency, representation-level drift as an early safety signal, and the governance advantage of lifecycle monitoring. For philosophers and governance specialists, it offers a vocabulary for specifying substrate-level monitoring obligations within existing governance frameworks. The morality-as-a-system framework does not displace elements such as constitutional AI or RLHF it embeds them within a larger temporal and structural account and specifies the additional infrastructure those methods require.

</details>


### [15] [Good for the Planet, Bad for Me? Intended and Unintended Consequences of AI Energy Consumption Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23075)
*Michael Klesel,Uwe Messer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 能量消耗披露能有效引导用户选择节能的小语言模型而非大语言模型，但会产生感知偏见和安慰剂效应，影响用户满意度和感知质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决人工智能的高能耗问题，研究者提出能量消耗披露来引导用户采取更可持续的实践，例如选择高效的小语言模型而非大语言模型，这为用户带来了性能与可持续性之间的权衡

Method: 通过365名参与者的实验，研究能量消耗披露的影响，以及选择小语言模型而非大语言模型的感知和行为后果

Result: 能量消耗披露是引导个体做出亲环境选择的高度有效措施，将选择节能小语言模型而非大语言模型的几率提高了12倍以上；但这一选择并未显著影响后续行为，选择小语言模型和大语言模型的个体表现出相似的提示行为；然而，选择产生了感知偏见，出现了安慰剂效应，选择"环保"小语言模型的个体报告了显著更低的满意度和感知质量

Conclusion: 能量消耗披露具有双重性，对可持续人机交互设计具有重要启示：虽然能有效引导环保选择，但可能产生感知偏见和安慰剂效应，影响用户体验

Abstract: To address the high energy consumption of artificial intelligence, energy consumption disclosure (ECD) has been proposed to steer users toward more sustainable practices, such as choosing efficient small language models (SLMs) over large language models (LLMs). This presents a performance-sustainability trade-off for users. In an experiment with 365 participants, we explore the impact of ECD and the perceptual and behavioral consequences of choosing an SLM over an LLM. Our findings reveal that ECD is a highly effective measure to nudge individuals toward a pro-environmental choice, increasing the odds of choosing an energy efficient SLM over an LLM by more than 12. Interestingly, this choice did not significantly impact subsequent behavior, as individuals who selected an SLM and those who selected an LLM demonstrated similar prompt behavior. Nevertheless, the choice created a perceptual bias. A placebo effect emerged, with individuals who selected the "eco-friendly" SLM reporting significantly lower satisfaction and perceived quality. These results highlight the double-edged nature of ECD, which holds critical implications for the design of sustainable human-computer interactions.

</details>


### [16] [Who Is in the Room? Stakeholder Perspectives on AI Recording in Pediatric Emergency Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23187)
*Alexandre De Masi,Sergio Manzano,Johan N. Siebert,Frederic Ehrler*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨儿科急诊中AI录音录像系统的设计缺失关键利益相关者视角，提出以利益相关者为中心的HCI研究框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿科急诊中新兴的AI录音录像系统作为HCI技术直接影响临床工作，但最受影响的临床医生、家长和儿童患者的视角在设计和治理中基本缺失，这直接影响系统的合法性和有效性

Method: 本文为立场论文，通过分析四个关键领域（知情同意、情感影响、监控动态、参与式治理）来探讨缺失视角的后果，并提出四个重新定位的立场

Result: 识别出四个缺失视角导致严重后果的领域：1) 知情同意在紧急情况下的复杂性；2) 录音录像对患者和家属的情感影响；3) 监控动态对临床工作的影响；4) 缺乏参与式治理机制

Conclusion: 提出四个重新定位立场：将AI录音录像系统转向以利益相关者为中心的HCI研究，确保临床医生、家长和儿童患者的视角在系统设计和治理中得到充分考虑，以提高系统的合法性和有效性

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems that record voice and video during pediatric emergencies are
  emerging as human-computer interaction (HCI) technologies with direct implications for clinical work,
  promising improvements in documentation, team performance, and post-event debriefing. Yet the
  perspectives of those most affected, including clinicians, parents, and child patients, remain largely
  absent from the design and governance of these technologies. This position paper argues that this has
  direct consequences for the legitimacy and effectiveness of these systems. We examine four areas where
  these missing perspectives prove consequential (consent, emotional impact, surveillance dynamics, and
  participatory governance) and propose four positions for reorienting AI recording in pediatric emergency
  care toward stakeholder-centered HCI inquiry.

</details>


### [17] [Design Space and Implementation of RAG-Based Avatars for Virtual Archaeology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23353)
*Wilhelm Kerle-Malcharek,Giulio Biondi,Karsten Klein,Ulf Hailer,Steffen Diefenbach,Fabrizio Grosso,Marco Legittimo,Paola Venuti,Carla Binucci,Giuseppe Liotta,Falk Schreiber*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在虚拟现实环境中使用基于检索增强生成（RAG）的对话式虚拟人物作为数字文化遗产的交互界面，以罗马马克森提乌斯陵墓为案例，评估了其信息获取效果和用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式技术（如虚拟现实和增强现实）正在改变数字文化遗产领域，但用户在与数字文化遗产对象交互时缺乏便捷获取深度信息的方式。研究旨在探索如何通过AI增强的虚拟人物提供按需的、经过策划的文化遗产信息，改善用户在沉浸式环境中的学习体验。

Method: 1. 提出了一个涵盖应用领域、虚拟人物个性和输入输出模式的需求设计空间；2. 开发了一个RAG系统，结合对话式虚拟人物在VR环境中实现；3. 以4世纪罗马马克森提乌斯陵墓为案例研究；4. 利用学术文本并添加元数据进行知识库构建；5. 评估不同RAG配置在专家设计问答集上的回答质量；6. 通过VR设置评估用户感知的工作负荷。

Result: 1. 用户感知与RAG虚拟人物交互的整体工作负荷低于平均水平；2. 此类虚拟人物有助于提升用户对主题的参与度；3. 展示了RAG驱动的VR虚拟人物在考古学应用中的可行性；4. 为沉浸式、AI增强的数字文化遗产应用提供了实证支持。

Conclusion: 基于RAG的VR虚拟人物为数字文化遗产提供了有效的交互界面，能够降低用户认知负荷并增强主题参与度。该研究为沉浸式AI增强文化遗产应用开辟了新途径，展示了将学术知识与沉浸式体验结合的潜力。

Abstract: Immersive technologies, such as virtual and augmented reality, are transforming digital heritage by enabling users to explore and interact with culturally significant sites. It is now possible to view and augment digital twins, or digitally reconstructed versions of them, and to enable access to previously unreachable locations for a broader audience. Here, we investigate retrieval-augmented generation (RAG)-based avatars as an interface for accessing further information about digital cultural heritage objects while immersed in dedicated virtual environments. We present a requirement design space that spans the application realm, avatar personality, and I/O modalities. We instantiate it with a RAG system coupled to a conversational avatar in a virtual reality (VR) environment, using the Maxentius mausoleum from the 4th century AD as a case study, through which users gain access to curated on-demand information of the digitised heritage object. Our workflow utilises scholarly texts and enriches them with metadata. We evaluate various RAG configurations in terms of answer quality on a small expert-crafted question-answer set, as well as the perceived workload of users of a VR setup using such a RAG avatar. We demonstrate evidence that users perceive the overall workload for interacting with such an avatar as below average and that such avatars help to gain topical engagement. Overall, our work demonstrates how to utilise RAG-driven VR avatars for archaeological purposes and provides evidence that they can offer a pathway for immersive, AI-enhanced digital heritage applications.

</details>


### [18] ["I Might be Using His... But It is Also Mine!": Ownership and Control in Accounts Designed for Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23391)
*Ji Eun Song,Jaeyoun You,Joongseek Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究分析了流媒体平台共享账户的所有权感知，识别了两种共享实践（随意共享和成本分摊）及相应的所有权类型（主要所有权和双重所有权），揭示了所有权感知不对称导致的冲突，并提出了基于共享实践的设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户对云文件等虚拟对象的所有权感知通常不确定，本研究旨在探讨流媒体平台中专门设计用于共享的账户（DS账户）是否也存在类似问题，并理解其共享实践中的所有权特征和潜在冲突。

Method: 采用两种混合方法研究：观察流媒体平台DS账户的共享实践，识别共享模式；通过实证研究分析不同共享实践下的所有权特征和用户感知差异。

Result: 识别出两种共享实践：随意共享（Casual，账户所有者单独支付）和成本分摊（Cost-splitting，配置文件持有者分摊费用）。每种实践中区分两种所有权类型：主要所有权（账户所有者有权允许他人使用）和双重所有权（主要所有权与共同所有权结合，用户间存在不对称的所有权感知）。当共享协议崩溃时会出现冲突。

Conclusion: 基于DS账户的共享实践，提出了弥合所有权差异的设计建议，以解决因所有权感知不对称导致的冲突问题，为流媒体平台共享账户的设计提供指导。

Abstract: A user's ownership perception of virtual objects, such as cloud files, is generally uncertain. Is this valid for streaming platforms featuring accounts designed for sharing (DS)? We observe sharing practices within DS accounts of streaming platforms and identify their ownership characteristics and unexpected complications through two mixed-method studies. Casual and Cost-splitting are the two sharing practices identified. The owner is the sole payer for the account in the former, whereas profile holders split the cost in the latter. We distinguish two types of ownership in each practice -- Primary and Dual. In Primary ownership, the account owner has the power to allow others to use the account; in Dual ownership, Primary ownership appears in conjunction with joint ownership, notably displaying asymmetric ownership perceptions among users. Conflicts arise when the sharing agreements collapse. Therefore, we propose design recommendations that bridge ownership differences based on sharing practices of DS accounts.

</details>

### [19] [Modeling Edge-to-Cloud Offloading Workloads for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23310)
*Longkun Li,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出自动驾驶车辆到云端数据传输的工作负载建模框架，基于真实移动轨迹分析三类数据（遥测、事件驱动车队学习、高精地图更新）的时空特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆产生大量数据用于车队监控、模型重训练和高精地图更新等应用，现有研究依赖通用交通轨迹，无法捕捉自动驾驶工作负载特性。

Method: 提出系统级工作负载建模框架，将卸载数据分为三类：遥测、事件驱动车队学习和高精地图更新，基于经验数据使用参数化公式建模其生成过程，并使用慕尼黑真实移动轨迹分析工作负载的时空分布。

Result: 结果显示工作负载规模随车辆渗透率增加，呈现时间结构和接入点间的空间不平衡，与基线交通模型有显著区别。

Conclusion: 该工作负载建模框架能更准确地捕捉自动驾驶车辆到云端数据传输的特性，为系统设计和优化提供基础。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles generate large volumes of data for applications such as fleet monitoring, model retraining, and high-definition map updates. Existing studies often rely on generic traffic traces, which do not capture the characteristics of autonomous driving workloads. This paper proposes a system-level workload modeling framework for vehicle-to-cloud data. We classify offloaded data into three types: telemetry, event-driven fleet learning, and high-definition map updates, while we model their generation using a parameterized formulation based on empirical data. Using a real-world mobility trace from Munich, we analyze the resulting workloads over time and space. The results show that workload scales with vehicle penetration, exhibits temporal structure and spatial imbalance across access points, and is distinguished from baseline traffic models.

</details>


### [20] [PNap: Lifecycle-aware Edge Multi-state sleep for Energy Efficient MEC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23323)
*Federico Giarrè,Holger Karl*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: PowerNap (PNap) 是一个生命周期感知的边缘计算编排框架，通过联合管理服务器睡眠状态和服务生命周期状态，在保证服务可用性的同时降低能耗


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多接入边缘计算（MEC）需要在多个位置保持边缘服务器运行以满足低延迟需求，即使在低负载阶段也会显著增加能耗。现有的多状态睡眠机制虽然能节省能源，但唤醒延迟较长，且服务执行依赖于无法在服务器睡眠时执行的生命周期操作，导致能源管理与服务连续性紧密耦合

Method: 提出PowerNap（PNap）框架，通过联合管理服务器睡眠状态和服务生命周期状态，利用流量预测技术，共同最小化活跃边缘服务器数量和服务中断

Result: PNap相比最先进的解决方案可减少高达14.9%的能耗，同时保持相同的服务可用性结果

Conclusion: PNap通过生命周期感知的编排方法，有效解决了边缘计算中能源效率与服务连续性之间的权衡问题，为边缘计算环境提供了实用的能源管理解决方案

Abstract: Multi-access Edge Computings (MECs) enables low-latency services by executing applications at the network edge. To fulfill low-latency requirements of mobile users, providers have to keep multiple edge servers running at multiple locations, even when, in low-load phases, their capacity is not needed. This significantly increases energy consumption. Multi-state sleep mechanisms mitigate this issue by allowing servers to enter progressively deeper sleep states, trading energy savings for longer wake-up delays. At the same time, service execution depends on non-instantaneous lifecycle operations that cannot be performed while servers are asleep, tightly coupling energy management with service continuity. This paper introduces PowerNap (PNap), a lifecycle-aware orchestration framework that jointly manages server sleep states and service lifecycle states. By leveraging traffic forecasting, PNap jointly minimizes the number of active edge servers and service disruptions. We compare PNap against baselines approaches and a state-of-the-art approach. Results validate PNap, showing how it can reduce energy consumption by up to 14.9% with respect to a state-of-the-art solution while matching its service availability results.

</details>


### [21] [Can NR-V2X Sidelink support A2A links?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23330)
*Vittorio Todisco,Alessandro Bazzi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文探讨将3GPP Release 18 Sidelink通信标准应用于空中实体间的空对空链路，分析传播延迟对长距离通信的影响，发现超过42.4公里时需要限制传输机会，但在此距离以下无需修改标准即可实现可行通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3GPP NR-V2X标准已扩展为通用的Sidelink通信，支持设备间直接通信。随着标准从车辆通信扩展到更广泛的应用场景，研究者希望探索该标准在空对空链路中的应用潜力，特别是在长距离通信场景下的可行性。

Method: 论文分析Release 18 Sidelink通信标准在自主资源分配模式下的工作机制，重点关注其应用于空对空链路时面临的挑战。通过理论分析传播延迟对同步资源调度的影响，识别关键限制因素，并提出必要的修改方案来适应公里级的长距离通信需求。

Result: 分析表明传播延迟是主要限制因素。通信距离超过42.4公里时，需要限制用户的传输机会以确保系统正常工作。然而，在42.4公里以下的距离范围内，现有的Sidelink通信标准无需任何修改即可支持空对空链路通信。

Conclusion: 3GPP Release 18 Sidelink通信标准在42.4公里距离内可有效支持空对空链路通信，无需修改标准。对于更长距离的通信，需要通过限制传输机会来适应传播延迟，这为未来标准演进提供了方向。

Abstract: In the context of 5G, 3GPP introduced New Radio vehicle to everything (NR-V2X) for direct vehicle-to-vehicle communication. However, starting from Release 18 the focus of the standard has been expanded from vehicles to any device and use case that can benefit from direct communication. In 3GPP terminology, the standard is now referred to simply as Sidelink communication. This standard allows direct communication between devices based on synchronous resource scheduling. Users can rely on controlled scheduling when in network coverage or, in the absence of coverage, autonomously select resources for transmission via a distributed resource allocation mechanism. Focusing on the autonomous resource allocation, this paper investigates the possibility of applying Release 18 Sidelink communication to Air-to-Air (A2A) links between airborne entities. The paper outlines the main challenges and required modifications to adapt the current standard for longer links in the order of kilometres. The analysis identifies the propagation delay as a critical limitation. Communications at distances over 42.4 km require a restriction of the user's transmitting opportunities. However, sidelink communication remains feasible for distances below this threshold without modifications to the standard.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [22] [Beyond Hard Constraints: Budget-Conditioned Reachability For Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22292)
*Janaka Chathuranga Brahmanage,Akshat Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于安全可达性分析的离线安全强化学习方法，通过定义安全条件可达集来解耦奖励最大化与累积安全成本约束，避免不稳定的min/max对抗优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界任务需要在奖励最大化与安全约束之间取得平衡，传统方法常导致不稳定的min/max对抗优化。现有可达性分析方法主要处理硬安全约束，很少扩展到累积成本约束。

Method: 1) 定义安全条件可达集，将奖励最大化与累积安全成本约束解耦；2) 展示该集合如何在不使用不稳定min/max或拉格朗日优化的情况下强制执行安全约束；3) 提出一种从固定数据集学习安全策略而无需环境交互的离线安全RL算法。

Result: 在标准离线安全RL基准测试和真实世界海上导航任务上的实验表明，该方法匹配或优于最先进的基线方法，同时保持安全性。

Conclusion: 通过安全条件可达集方法，成功解决了累积安全成本约束问题，避免了传统对抗优化的不稳定性，为离线安全RL提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Sequential decision making using Markov Decision Process underpins many realworld applications. Both model-based and model free methods have achieved strong results in these settings. However, real-world tasks must balance reward maximization with safety constraints, often conflicting objectives, that can lead to unstable min/max, adversarial optimization. A promising alternative is safety reachability analysis, which precomputes a forward-invariant safe state, action set, ensuring that an agent starting inside this set remains safe indefinitely. Yet, most reachability based methods address only hard safety constraints, and little work extends reachability to cumulative cost constraints. To address this, first, we define a safetyconditioned reachability set that decouples reward maximization from cumulative safety cost constraints. Second, we show how this set enforces safety constraints without unstable min/max or Lagrangian optimization, yielding a novel offline safe RL algorithm that learns a safe policy from a fixed dataset without environment interaction. Finally, experiments on standard offline safe RL benchmarks, and a real world maritime navigation task demonstrate that our method matches or outperforms state of the art baselines while maintaining safety.

</details>


### [23] [Efficient Embedding-based Synthetic Data Generation for Complex Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22294)
*Srideepika Jayaraman,Achille Fokoue,Dhaval Patel,Jayant Kalagnanam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于嵌入空间分析的合成数据生成方法，通过嵌入空间采样增强数据多样性，提升小模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据生成（SDG）利用大语言模型（LLM）为小模型提供微调数据，但面临数据质量和多样性的挑战。研究发现嵌入空间中特定邻域内样本密度与该区域预测准确性存在强相关性，这为解决数据多样性问题提供了新视角。

Method: 提出基于嵌入的目标采样流水线：1）分析生成数据在嵌入空间的分布和多样性；2）利用嵌入空间中样本密度与预测准确性的相关性；3）设计针对性的嵌入空间采样策略来增强数据多样性。

Result: 该方法在多个基准测试中一致提升了性能，证明了嵌入空间采样策略在增强数据多样性和改善模型性能方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间分析为合成数据生成的质量控制提供了有效框架，基于嵌入的采样方法能够系统性地增强数据多样性，从而提升小语言模型在微调后的性能表现。

Abstract: Synthetic Data Generation (SDG), leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs), has recently been recognized and broadly adopted as an effective approach to improve the performance of smaller but more resource and compute efficient LLMs through fine-tuning. A key challenge in SDG is ensuring the quality and diversity of the generated data. In this paper, we analyze the diversity and distribution of generated data in the embedding space, and demonstrate a strong correlation between the density of examples within a specific neighborhood and the accuracy of predictions on examples drawn from that region. Building on this insight, we present a targeted pipeline for embedding-based sampling that enhances data diversity and consistently improves performance across several benchmarks.

</details>


### [24] [Between the Layers Lies the Truth: Uncertainty Estimation in LLMs Using Intra-Layer Local Information Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22299)
*Zvi N. Badash,Yonatan Belinkov,Moti Freiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于内部表示跨层一致性模式的轻量级不确定性估计方法，在保持性能的同时显著提升了跨数据集和量化场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型经常表现出"自信的错误"，需要可靠的不确定性估计。现有方法存在局限性：基于输出的启发式方法廉价但脆弱，而探测内部表示的方法虽然有效但维度高且难以迁移。

Method: 提出了一种紧凑的、基于实例的不确定性估计方法，通过单次前向传播对内部表示的跨层一致性模式进行评分。该方法分析不同层之间的表示一致性来评估模型的不确定性。

Result: 在三个模型上的实验表明：1) 在分布内测试中，与探测方法性能相当（AUPRC差异最大-1.8个百分点，Brier分数差异+4.9分）；2) 在跨数据集迁移中，始终优于探测方法（AUPRC提升最高+2.86，Brier分数提升最高+21.02）；3) 在4位权重量化下保持鲁棒性，平均提升+1.94 AUPRC和+5.33 Brier分数。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种轻量级、紧凑的方式来捕获LLMs中可迁移的不确定性。通过分析特定层间交互，揭示了不同模型编码不确定性的差异，为理解模型内部不确定性表示提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often confidently wrong, making reliable uncertainty estimation (UE) essential. Output-based heuristics are cheap but brittle, while probing internal representations is effective yet high-dimensional and hard to transfer.
  We propose a compact, per-instance UE method that scores cross-layer agreement patterns in internal representations using a single forward pass.
  Across three models, our method matches probing in-distribution, with mean diagonal differences of at most $-1.8$ AUPRC percentage points and $+4.9$ Brier score points. Under cross-dataset transfer, it consistently outperforms probing, achieving off-diagonal gains up to $+2.86$ AUPRC and $+21.02$ Brier points. Under 4-bit weight-only quantization, it remains robust, improving over probing by $+1.94$ AUPRC points and $+5.33$ Brier points on average.
  Beyond performance, examining specific layer--layer interactions reveals differences in how disparate models encode uncertainty. Altogether, our UE method offers a lightweight, compact means to capture transferable uncertainty in LLMs.

</details>


### [25] [Scaling Attention via Feature Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22300)
*Yan Xie,Tiansheng Wen,Tangda Huang,Bo Chen,Chenyu You,Stefanie Jegelka,Yifei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sparse Feature Attention (SFA) 通过特征稀疏性将注意力计算成本从 O(n²d) 降低到 O(n²k²/d)，在保持准确性的同时显著提升效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过序列轴（局部窗口、核近似、令牌级稀疏）减少注意力计算成本，但这些方法会降低准确性。本文探索正交的特征稀疏性轴，以在保持高维表达能力的同时降低计算成本

Method: 提出 Sparse Feature Attention (SFA)，将查询和键表示为 k-稀疏编码，保留高维表达能力。开发 FlashSFA，一个 IO 感知的内核，扩展 FlashAttention 直接在稀疏重叠上操作，无需物化密集分数矩阵

Result: 在 GPT-2 和 Qwen3 预训练中，SFA 匹配密集基线，同时速度提升高达 2.5 倍，FLOPs 和 KV-cache 减少近 50%。在合成和下游基准测试中，SFA 在长上下文中保持检索准确性和鲁棒性，优于特征多样性崩溃的短嵌入基线

Conclusion: 特征级稀疏性是高效注意力的互补且未充分探索的轴，使 Transformer 能够以最小质量损失扩展到数量级更长的上下文

Abstract: Scaling Transformers to ultra-long contexts is bottlenecked by the $O(n^2 d)$ cost of self-attention. Existing methods reduce this cost along the sequence axis through local windows, kernel approximations, or token-level sparsity, but these approaches consistently degrade accuracy. In this paper, we instead explore an orthogonal axis: feature sparsity. We propose Sparse Feature Attention (SFA), where queries and keys are represented as $k$-sparse codes that preserve high-dimensional expressivity while reducing the cost of attention from $Θ(n^2 d)$ to $Θ(n^2 k^2/d)$. To make this efficient at scale, we introduce FlashSFA, an IO-aware kernel that extends FlashAttention to operate directly on sparse overlaps without materializing dense score matrices. Across GPT-2 and Qwen3 pretraining, SFA matches dense baselines while improving speed by up to $2.5\times$ and reducing FLOPs and KV-cache by nearly 50\%. On synthetic and downstream benchmarks, SFA preserves retrieval accuracy and robustness at long contexts, outperforming short-embedding baselines that collapse feature diversity. These results establish feature-level sparsity as a complementary and underexplored axis for efficient attention, enabling Transformers to scale to orders-of-magnitude longer contexts with minimal quality loss. Code is available at https://github.com/YannX1e/Sparse-Feature-Attention.

</details>


### [26] [Latent Semantic Manifolds in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22301)
*Mohamed A. Mabrok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出几何框架将LLM隐藏状态解释为潜在语义流形上的点，证明词汇离散化导致表达性间隙，验证了跨模型的通用几何模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在连续向量空间中进行内部计算却产生离散标记，这种基本不匹配的几何后果尚未被充分理解。需要建立数学框架来分析隐藏状态的几何结构及其与词汇离散化的关系。

Method: 开发数学框架将LLM隐藏状态解释为黎曼子流形上的点，使用Fisher信息度量，标记对应流形的Voronoi区域。定义表达性间隙衡量语义失真，证明两个定理：有限词汇的率失真下界和通过余面积公式的线性体积缩放定律。在6个transformer架构（124M-1.5B参数）上验证预测。

Result: 验证了通用沙漏形内在维度剖面、平滑曲率结构和线性间隙缩放（斜率0.87-1.12，R^2 > 0.985）。边界邻近表示的硬核分布在模型规模变化时保持稳定，提供了困惑度的几何分解。

Conclusion: 该几何框架揭示了LLM隐藏状态的深层结构，表达性间隙的线性缩放定律和通用几何模式对架构设计、模型压缩、解码策略和缩放定律具有重要启示。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) perform internal computations in continuous vector spaces yet produce discrete tokens -- a fundamental mismatch whose geometric consequences remain poorly understood. We develop a mathematical framework that interprets LLM hidden states as points on a latent semantic manifold: a Riemannian submanifold equipped with the Fisher information metric, where tokens correspond to Voronoi regions partitioning the manifold. We define the expressibility gap, a geometric measure of the semantic distortion from vocabulary discretization, and prove two theorems: a rate-distortion lower bound on distortion for any finite vocabulary, and a linear volume scaling law for the expressibility gap via the coarea formula. We validate these predictions across six transformer architectures (124M-1.5B parameters), confirming universal hourglass intrinsic dimension profiles, smooth curvature structure, and linear gap scaling with slopes 0.87-1.12 (R^2 > 0.985). The margin distribution across models reveals a persistent hard core of boundary-proximal representations invariant to scale, providing a geometric decomposition of perplexity. We discuss implications for architecture design, model compression, decoding strategies, and scaling laws

</details>


### [27] [Research on Individual Trait Clustering and Development Pathway Adaptation Based on the K-means Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22302)
*Qianru Wei,Jihaoyu Yang,Cheng Zhang,Jinming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究使用K-means聚类算法分析大学生特征数据，为不同特征组合的学生群体提供精准的职业指导建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有职业指导方法多关注职业路径预测，但缺乏对不同特征组合学生在特定职业方向适应性的研究。随着信息技术发展，人工智能和机器学习在教育领域应用潜力巨大，需要更科学的个性化职业指导方法。

Method: 收集3000多名学生的CET-4成绩、GPA、性格特征和学生干部经历数据，采用K-means聚类算法将学生分为四个主要群体。该算法通过最小化簇内平方误差，将具有相似特征的学生归为一组，确保同一簇内学生特征高度相似，不同簇间差异最大化。

Result: 研究结果显示，具有不同特征组合的学生适合不同的职业方向。聚类分析为个性化职业指导提供了科学依据，有效提升了学生的就业成功率。

Conclusion: K-means聚类算法能够有效识别学生特征模式，为精准职业指导提供支持。未来研究可通过扩大样本规模、增加特征变量和考虑外部因素，进一步提升聚类精度和指导效果。

Abstract: With the development of information technology, the application of artificial intelligence and machine learning in the field of education shows great potential. This study aims to explore how to utilize K-means clustering algorithm to provide accurate career guidance for college students. Existing methods mostly focus on the prediction of career paths, but there are fewer studies on the fitness of students with different combinations of characteristics in specific career directions. In this study, we analyze the data of more than 3000 students on their CET-4 scores, GPA, personality traits and student cadre experiences, and use the K-means clustering algorithm to classify the students into four main groups. The K-means clustering algorithm groups students with similar characteristics into one group by minimizing the intra-cluster squared error, ensuring that the students within the same cluster are highly similar in their characteristics, and that differences between different clusters are maximized. Based on the clustering results, targeted career guidance suggestions are provided for each group. The results of the study show that students with different combinations of characteristics are suitable for different career directions, which provides a scientific basis for personalized career guidance and effectively enhances students' employment success rate. Future research can further improve the precision of clustering and the guidance effect by expanding the sample size, increasing the feature variables and considering external factors.

</details>


### [28] [Sample Transform Cost-Based Training-Free Hallucination Detector for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22303)
*Zeyang Ding,Xinglin Hu,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于最优传输距离的幻觉检测方法，通过计算样本间Wasserstein距离矩阵来量化LLM生成分布复杂度，无需训练即可检测幻觉


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型中的幻觉问题是可信部署的主要障碍，需要准确、轻量且广泛适用的检测器。作者认为LLM在给定提示下定义的分布复杂度是幻觉的指标，但分布密度未知且样本是离散分布，量化复杂度面临挑战

Method: 计算成对样本的token嵌入集合之间的最优传输距离，得到Wasserstein距离矩阵来量化分布复杂度。基于该矩阵推导两个互补信号：AvgWD（平均成本）和EigenWD（成本复杂度）。还通过教师强制方法将框架扩展到黑盒LLM

Result: 实验表明AvgWD和EigenWD与强不确定性基线具有竞争力，在不同模型和数据集上表现出互补行为，证明分布复杂度是LLM真实性的有效信号

Conclusion: 分布复杂度是检测LLM幻觉的有效指标，提出的基于Wasserstein距离矩阵的无训练检测器在准确性和适用性方面表现良好，为可信LLM部署提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Hallucinations in large language models (LLMs) remain a central obstacle to trustworthy deployment, motivating detectors that are accurate, lightweight, and broadly applicable. Since an LLM with a prompt defines a conditional distribution, we argue that the complexity of the distribution is an indicator of hallucination. However, the density of the distribution is unknown and the samples (i.e., responses generated for the prompt) are discrete distributions, which leads to a significant challenge in quantifying the complexity of the distribution. We propose to compute the optimal-transport distances between the sets of token embeddings of pairwise samples, which yields a Wasserstein distance matrix measuring the costs of transforming between the samples. This Wasserstein distance matrix provides a means to quantify the complexity of the distribution defined by the LLM with the prompt. Based on the Wasserstein distance matrix, we derive two complementary signals: AvgWD, measuring the average cost, and EigenWD, measuring the cost complexity. This leads to a training-free detector for hallucinations in LLMs. We further extend the framework to black-box LLMs via teacher forcing with an accessible teacher model. Experiments show that AvgWD and EigenWD are competitive with strong uncertainty baselines and provide complementary behavior across models and datasets, highlighting distribution complexity as an effective signal for LLM truthfulness.

</details>


### [29] [Mitigating Premature Discretization with Progressive Quantization for Robust Vector Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22304)
*Wenhao Zhao,Qiran Zou,Zhouhan Lin,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProVQ提出渐进量化方法，通过将量化硬度作为训练轴，从连续潜在空间平滑退火到离散空间，解决了现有VQ方法中过早离散化的问题，在图像和生物序列建模中均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量量化(VQ)方法存在过早离散化问题：在编码器捕获底层数据流形之前就强制离散化，导致量化效果不佳。这种过早离散化限制了VQ在多模态大语言模型和扩散合成中的表现。

Method: 提出渐进量化(ProVQ)，将量化硬度作为VQ训练中一个基本但先前被忽视的轴。通过将量化视为从连续潜在空间平滑退火到离散空间的课程，引导码本朝向充分展开的流形。量化硬度在训练过程中逐渐增加，使编码器能够先学习数据分布，再逐步引入离散化约束。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K和ImageNet-100基准测试中，ProVQ显著提升了重建和生成性能。在蛋白质结构标记化任务中，ProVQ在StrutTokenBench排行榜上建立了新的性能上限，证明了对复杂生物序列建模的高度有效性。

Conclusion: ProVQ通过引入渐进量化机制，有效解决了VQ中的过早离散化问题，为多模态表示学习提供了更优的量化范式，在图像和生物序列等多种模态上都展现出广泛的有效性。

Abstract: Vector Quantization (VQ) has become the cornerstone of tokenization for many multimodal Large Language Models and diffusion synthesis. However, existing VQ paradigms suffer from a fundamental conflict: they enforce discretization before the encoder has captured the underlying data manifold. We term this phenomenon Premature Discretization. To resolve this, we propose Progressive Quantization (ProVQ), which incorporates the dynamics of quantization hardness as a fundamental yet previously overlooked axis in VQ training. By treating quantization as a curriculum that smoothly anneals from a continuous latent space to a discrete one, ProVQ effectively guides the codebook toward the well-expanded manifolds. Extensive experimental results demonstrate the broad effectiveness of ProVQ across diverse modalities. We report improved reconstruction and generative performance on the ImageNet-1K and ImageNet-100 benchmarks, highlighting the ProVQ's boost for generative modeling. Furthermore, ProVQ proves highly effective for modeling complex biological sequences, establishing a new performance ceiling for protein structure tokenization on the StrutTokenBench leaderboard.

</details>


### [30] [Behavioral Heterogeneity as Quantum-Inspired Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22729)
*Mohammad Elayan,Wissam Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种量子启发的驾驶员异质性表示方法，将驾驶员建模为密度矩阵形式的动态潜在状态，通过非线性随机傅里叶特征嵌入行为观测，结合时间持续性和上下文依赖的配置文件激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将驾驶员异质性简化为标签或离散状态，将本质上动态的行为压缩为静态类别，这种简化无法充分捕捉驾驶员行为的连续动态特性。

Method: 采用量子启发的表示方法，将每个驾驶员建模为密度矩阵形式的演化潜在状态。使用非线性随机傅里叶特征嵌入行为观测，状态演化结合了行为的时间持续性和上下文依赖的配置文件激活机制。

Result: 在经验驾驶数据（TGSIM第三代仿真数据）上评估了该方法，展示了如何提取和分析驾驶配置文件，验证了量子启发表示的有效性。

Conclusion: 量子启发的密度矩阵表示能够更好地捕捉驾驶员行为的动态异质性，相比传统的静态分类方法，提供了更丰富和连续的行为建模框架。

Abstract: Driver heterogeneity is often reduced to labels or discrete regimes, compressing what is inherently dynamic into static categories. We introduce quantum-inspired representation that models each driver as an evolving latent state, presented as a density matrix with structured mathematical properties. Behavioral observations are embedded via non-linear Random Fourier Features, while state evolution blends temporal persistence of behavior with context-dependent profile activation. We evaluate our approach on empirical driving data, Third Generation Simulation Data (TGSIM), showing how driving profiles are extracted and analyzed.

</details>


### [31] [A Multi-Modal CNN-LSTM Framework with Multi-Head Attention and Focal Loss for Real-Time Elderly Fall Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22313)
*Lijie Zhou,Luran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多模态深度学习框架MultiModalFallDetector，用于老年人实时跌倒检测，融合加速度计、陀螺仪和生理信号，在SisFall数据集上达到F1分数98.7，推理延迟低于50ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球老龄化加剧了对可靠健康监测系统的需求，特别是老年人跌倒检测。传统单模态加速度方法误报率高，传统机器学习需要大量手工特征工程。

Method: 提出MultiModalFallDetector框架，包含：多尺度CNN特征提取器捕捉不同时间分辨率的运动动态；融合三轴加速度计、陀螺仪和四通道生理信号；多头自注意力机制进行动态时间加权；采用Focal Loss缓解类别不平衡；引入辅助活动分类任务进行正则化；实施从UCI HAR到SisFall数据集的迁移学习。

Result: 在SisFall数据集（包含60-85岁老年人真实模拟跌倒试验）上，框架达到F1分数98.7、召回率98.9、AUC-ROC 99.4，显著优于传统机器学习和标准深度学习方法。在边缘设备上保持低于50ms的推理延迟。

Conclusion: 提出的多模态深度学习框架在老年人跌倒检测中表现出色，高精度和低延迟使其适合老年护理环境中的实时部署。

Abstract: The increasing global aging population has intensified the demand for reliable health monitoring systems, particularly those capable of detecting critical events such as falls among elderly individuals. Traditional fall detection approaches relying on single-modality acceleration data suffer from high false alarm rates, while conventional machine learning methods require extensive hand-crafted feature engineering. This paper proposes a novel multi-modal deep learning framework, MultiModalFallDetector, designed for real-time elderly fall detection using wearable sensors. Our approach integrates multiple innovations: a multi-scale CNN-based feature extractor capturing motion dynamics at varying temporal resolutions; fusion of tri-axial accelerometer, gyroscope, and four-channel physiological signals; incorporation of a multi-head self-attention mechanism for dynamic temporal weighting; adoption of Focal Loss to mitigate severe class imbalance; introduction of an auxiliary activity classification task for regularization; and implementation of transfer learning from UCI HAR to SisFall dataset. Extensive experiments on the SisFall dataset, which includes real-world simulated fall trials from elderly participants (aged 60-85), demonstrate that our framework achieves an F1-score of 98. 7, Recall of 98. 9, and AUC-ROC of 99. 4, significantly outperforming baseline methods including traditional machine learning and standard deep learning approaches. The model maintains sub- 50ms inference latency on edge devices, confirming its suitability for real-time deployment in geriatric care settings.

</details>


### [32] [Emergency Preemption Without Online Exploration: A Decision Transformer Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22315)
*Haoran Su,Hanxiao Deng,Yandong Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于决策变换器的应急走廊优化框架，通过离线序列建模实现紧急车辆响应时间优化，支持单标量控制紧急程度，并扩展到多智能体设置


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有应急车辆信号优先策略反应式且不可控，响应时间是生存结果的关键决定因素，需要更智能、可控的优化方法

Method: 使用决策变换器将走廊优化建模为离线、回报条件序列建模，消除策略学习中的在线环境交互；通过单目标回报标量实现调度级紧急程度控制；扩展到多智能体决策变换器，使用图注意力进行空间协调

Result: 在LightSim模拟器上，DT相比固定时序优先策略减少37.7%的平均EV旅行时间；MADT在更大网格上进一步改进，实现45.2%的减少；回报条件产生平滑调度接口，无需重新训练即可在EV旅行时间和民用延迟之间权衡

Conclusion: 决策变换器框架为应急走廊优化提供了离线、可控的解决方案，通过回报条件实现紧急程度调节，多智能体扩展增强了大规模协调能力，为实际部署提供了实用接口

Abstract: Emergency vehicle (EV) response time is a critical determinant of survival outcomes, yet deployed signal preemption strategies remain reactive and uncontrollable. We propose a return-conditioned framework for emergency corridor optimization based on the Decision Transformer (DT). By casting corridor optimization as offline, return-conditioned sequence modeling, our approach (1) eliminates online environment interaction during policy learning, (2) enables dispatch-level urgency control through a single target-return scalar, and (3) extends to multi-agent settings via a Multi-Agent Decision Transformer (MADT) with graph attention for spatial coordination. On the LightSim simulator, DT reduces average EV travel time by 37.7% relative to fixed-timing preemption on a 4x4 grid (88.6 s vs. 142.3 s), achieving the lowest civilian delay (11.3 s/veh) and fewest EV stops (1.2) among all methods, including online RL baselines that require environment interaction. MADT further improves on larger grids, overtaking DT with 45.2% reduction on 8x8 via graph-attention coordination. Return conditioning produces a smooth dispatch interface: varying the target return from 100 to -400 trades EV travel time (72.4-138.2 s) against civilian delay (16.8-5.4 s/veh), requiring no retraining. A Constrained DT extension adds explicit civilian disruption budgets as a second control knob.

</details>


### [33] [Cloud-Edge Collaborative Large Models for Robust Photovoltaic Power Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22343)
*Nan Qiao,Sijing Duan,Shuning Wang,Xingyuan Hua,Ju Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出风险感知的云边协同光伏功率预测框架，平衡预测精度、鲁棒性和延迟约束


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决边缘电网中光伏功率预测面临的挑战：本地专用模型在常规条件下高效，但在罕见爬坡事件和未见天气模式下性能下降；完全依赖云端大模型则带来显著通信延迟和云端开销

Method: 提出风险感知的云边协同框架，包含：站点专用专家预测器处理常规情况；轻量级边缘侧模型增强本地推理；云端大型检索模型通过检索-预测管道提供匹配的历史上下文。轻量级筛选模块评估预测不确定性、分布外风险、天气突变强度和模型分歧，Lyapunov引导的路由器在长期延迟、通信和云端使用约束下选择性将推理升级到边缘小模型或云端辅助分支，激活分支的输出通过自适应融合组合

Result: 在两个真实世界光伏数据集上的实验表明，该框架在预测精度、路由质量、鲁棒性和系统效率之间取得了良好的整体权衡

Conclusion: 提出的风险感知云边协同框架有效解决了延迟敏感光伏预测中的精度-鲁棒性-效率权衡问题，通过智能路由和自适应融合机制实现了系统性能的优化

Abstract: Photovoltaic (PV) power forecasting in edge-enabled grids requires balancing forecasting accuracy, robustness under weather-driven distribution shifts, and strict latency constraints. Local specialized models are efficient for routine conditions but often degrade under rare ramp events and unseen weather patterns, whereas always relying on cloud-side large models incurs substantial communication delay and cloud overhead. To address this challenge, we propose a risk-aware cloud-edge collaborative framework for latency-sensitive PV forecasting. The framework integrates a site-specific expert predictor for routine cases, a lightweight edge-side model for enhanced local inference, and a cloud-side large retrieval model that provides matched historical context when needed through a retrieval-prediction pipeline. A lightweight screening module estimates predictive uncertainty, out-of-distribution risk, weather mutation intensity, and model disagreement, while a Lyapunov-guided router selectively escalates inference to the edge-small or cloud-assisted branches under long-term latency, communication, and cloud-usage constraints. The outputs of the activated branches are combined through adaptive fusion. Experiments on two real-world PV datasets demonstrate a favorable overall trade-off among forecasting accuracy, routing quality, robustness, and system efficiency.

</details>


### [34] [ST-GDance++: A Scalable Spatial-Temporal Diffusion for Long-Duration Group Choreography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22316)
*Jing Xu,Weiqiang Wang,Cunjian Chen,Jun Liu,Qiuhong Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ST-GDance++：一个可扩展的群体舞蹈生成框架，通过解耦时空依赖实现高效、碰撞感知的编舞生成，显著降低延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群体舞蹈生成模型存在双向注意力依赖问题，随着舞者数量和序列长度增加，注意力计算呈二次增长，导致效率降低和运动碰撞风险增加，难以部署到交互场景中

Method: 提出ST-GDance++框架，解耦空间和时间依赖：空间建模采用轻量级距离感知图卷积捕捉舞者间关系；时间建模设计扩散噪声调度策略和高效时间对齐注意力掩码，支持长序列流式生成

Result: 在AIOZ-GDance数据集上实验表明，ST-GDance++在保持竞争力的生成质量的同时，相比现有方法显著降低了延迟

Conclusion: ST-GDance++通过解耦时空依赖和高效建模策略，解决了群体舞蹈生成中的可扩展性和碰撞问题，为交互式应用提供了可行的解决方案

Abstract: Group dance generation from music requires synchronizing multiple dancers while maintaining spatial coordination, making it highly relevant to applications such as film production, gaming, and animation. Recent group dance generation models have achieved promising generation quality, but they remain difficult to deploy in interactive scenarios due to bidirectional attention dependencies. As the number of dancers and the sequence length increase, the attention computation required for aligning music conditions with motion sequences grows quadratically, leading to reduced efficiency and increased risk of motion collisions. Effectively modeling dense spatial-temporal interactions is therefore essential, yet existing methods often struggle to capture such complexity, resulting in limited scalability and unstable multi-dancer coordination. To address these challenges, we propose ST-GDance++, a scalable framework that decouples spatial and temporal dependencies to enable efficient and collision-aware group choreography generation. For spatial modeling, we introduce lightweight distance-aware graph convolutions to capture inter-dancer relationships while reducing computational overhead. For temporal modeling, we design a diffusion noise scheduling strategy together with an efficient temporal-aligned attention mask, enabling stream-based generation for long motion sequences and improving scalability in long-duration scenarios. Experiments on the AIOZ-GDance dataset show that ST-GDance++ achieves competitive generation quality with significantly reduced latency compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [35] [A Theoretical Framework for Energy-Aware Gradient Pruning in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22465)
*Emmanouil M. Athanasakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出成本加权幅度剪枝（CWMP）方法，通过考虑硬件成本差异来优化联邦学习的能耗效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习受限于边缘设备的通信和能耗限制，传统的Top-K幅度剪枝方法假设所有参数更新具有相同的传输和内存更新成本，忽略了硬件层面的实际差异

Method: 将剪枝过程形式化为能量约束的投影问题，考虑后向传播阶段内存密集型与计算高效操作之间的硬件差异，提出成本加权幅度剪枝（CWMP）选择规则，基于参数幅度相对于其物理成本进行优先级排序

Result: CWMP是该约束投影问题的最优贪婪解，在非独立同分布CIFAR-10基准测试中，CWMP相比Top-K基线始终建立了更优的性能-能量帕累托前沿

Conclusion: 通过考虑硬件成本差异的成本加权剪枝方法能够显著提升联邦学习的能量效率，为实际部署提供了更实用的解决方案

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is constrained by the communication and energy limitations of decentralized edge devices. While gradient sparsification via Top-K magnitude pruning effectively reduces the communication payload, it remains inherently energy-agnostic. It assumes all parameter updates incur identical downstream transmission and memory-update costs, ignoring hardware realities. We formalize the pruning process as an energy-constrained projection problem that accounts for the hardware-level disparities between memory-intensive and compute-efficient operations during the post-backpropagation phase. We propose Cost-Weighted Magnitude Pruning (CWMP), a selection rule that prioritizes parameter updates based on their magnitude relative to their physical cost. We demonstrate that CWMP is the optimal greedy solution to this constrained projection and provide a probabilistic analysis of its global energy efficiency. Numerical results on a non-IID CIFAR-10 benchmark show that CWMP consistently establishes a superior performance-energy Pareto frontier compared to the Top-K baseline.

</details>


### [36] [Bridging the Gap Between Climate Science and Machine Learning in Climate Model Emulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22320)
*Luca Schmidt,Nina Effenberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一个框架，通过整合气候科学和机器学习视角，解决气候模型ML模拟器在实际应用中面临的障碍，包括可访问性、专业知识缺乏和对ML方法物理可信度的不信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候模型虽然为气候决策提供重要见解，但其使用受到显著计算和技术要求的限制。机器学习模拟器可以规避高计算成本，但实际应用仍面临多重障碍：可访问性有限、缺乏专业知识、以及对被认为物理基础不足的ML方法普遍存在不信任。

Method: 引入一个整合气候科学和机器学习视角的框架。该框架强调设计易于采用的模拟器，这些模拟器针对明确定义的任务，并通过展示其可靠性来建立信任。

Result: 研究发现，设计易于采用的模拟器并展示其可靠性，为解决气候科学和机器学习领域之间的鸿沟提供了一条有前景的路径。

Conclusion: 通过整合气候科学和机器学习视角，并专注于开发易于采用且可靠的模拟器，可以有效克服当前气候模型ML模拟器应用中的障碍，促进两个领域的融合。

Abstract: While climate models provide insights for climate decision-making, their use is constrained by significant computational and technical demands. Although machine learning (ML) emulators offer a way to bypass the high computational costs, their effective use remains challenging. The hurdles are diverse, ranging from limited accessibility and a lack of specialized knowledge to a general mistrust of ML methods that are perceived as insufficiently physical. Here, we introduce a framework to overcome these barriers by integrating both climate science and machine learning perspectives. We find that designing easy-to-adopt emulators that address a clearly defined task and demonstrating their reliability offers a promising path for bridging the gap between our two fields.

</details>


### [37] [AEGIS: An Operational Infrastructure for Post-Market Governance of Adaptive Medical AI Under US and EU Regulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22322)
*Fardin Afdideh,Mehdi Astaraki,Fernando Seoane,Farhad Abtahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AEGIS是一个医疗AI治理框架，将FDA PCCP和欧盟AI法案条款转化为可执行程序，支持安全持续学习，包含数据集同化与重训练、模型监控、条件决策三个模块，采用四类部署决策分类和独立PMS ALARM信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗设备中部署的机器学习系统需要治理框架来确保安全同时支持持续改进。FDA和欧盟等监管机构引入了PCCP和PMS等机制来管理迭代模型更新而无需重复提交，但需要将这些监管概念转化为可执行的治理程序。

Method: 提出AEGIS治理框架，包含三个模块：数据集同化与重训练、模型监控、条件决策。采用四类部署决策分类（批准、有条件批准、临床审查、拒绝）和独立PMS ALARM信号。框架可跨异构临床环境实例化，通过两个示例（脓毒症预测和脑肿瘤分割）展示实施。

Result: 在脓毒症预测的11次模拟迭代中，AEGIS产生8次批准、1次有条件批准、1次临床审查和1次拒绝决策，覆盖所有四类决策。ALARM信号在第8和10次迭代发出，包括无可用模型且已发布模型同时失效的关键状态。AEGIS在可观察性能下降前检测到漂移。

Conclusion: AEGIS成功将监管变更控制概念转化为可执行的治理程序，支持跨不同临床应用的适应性医疗AI的安全持续学习，能够在性能明显下降前检测漂移并管理关键状态。

Abstract: Machine learning systems deployed in medical devices require governance frameworks that ensure safety while enabling continuous improvement. Regulatory bodies including the FDA and European Union have introduced mechanisms such as the Predetermined Change Control Plan (PCCP) and Post-Market Surveillance (PMS) to manage iterative model updates without repeated submissions. This paper presents AI/ML Evaluation and Governance Infrastructure for Safety (AEGIS), a governance framework applicable to any healthcare AI system. AEGIS comprises three modules, i.e., dataset assimilation and retraining, model monitoring, and conditional decision, that operationalize FDA PCCP and EU AI Act Article 43(4) provisions. We implement a four-category deployment decision taxonomy (APPROVE, CONDITIONAL APPROVAL, CLINICAL REVIEW, REJECT) with an independent PMS ALARM signal, enabling detection of the critical state in which no deployable model exists while the released model is simultaneously at risk.
  To illustrate how AEGIS can be instantiated across heterogeneous clinical contexts, we provide two examples: sepsis prediction from electronic health records and brain tumor segmentation from medical imaging. Both cases use identical governance architecture, differing only in configuration. Across 11 simulated iterations on the sepsis example, AEGIS yielded 8 APPROVE, 1 CONDITIONAL APPROVAL, 1 CLINICAL REVIEW, and 1 REJECT decision, exercising all four categories. ALARM signals were co-issued at iterations 8 and 10, including the critical state where no deployable model exists and the released model is simultaneously failing. AEGIS detected drift before observable performance degradation. These results demonstrate that AEGIS translates regulatory change-control concepts into executable governance procedures, supporting safe continuous learning for adaptive medical AI across diverse clinical applications.

</details>


### [38] [DAQ: Delta-Aware Quantization for Post-Training LLM Weight Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22324)
*Xiaoming Yu,Shize Tang,Guanghua Yu,Linchuan Xie,Song Liu,Jianchen Zhu,Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Delta-Aware Quantization (DAQ) 是一种无需数据的训练后量化框架，通过直接优化参数变化量的方向保真度来保护训练后获得的知识，相比标准量化方法能更好地保留模型特定能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量化方法最小化重构误差，但对基础模型不敏感，导致量化噪声不成比例地破坏编码训练后行为的小幅度参数变化量(ΔW)。这些变化量虽然幅度小，但包含了重要的训练后知识，标准量化会损害模型的特定能力。

Method: DAQ 使用两种变化量感知指标替代重构目标：符号保持率和余弦相似度。这两种指标直接优化ΔW的方向保真度，仅需要基础模型和训练后模型的权重矩阵，无需额外数据。

Result: 在FP8量化研究中，DAQ恢复了标准量化下丢失的特定风格能力，同时保持了一般性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过直接优化参数变化量的方向保真度，DAQ能够有效保护训练后获得的知识，在量化过程中更好地保留模型的特定能力，为训练后量化提供了新的优化视角。

Abstract: We introduce Delta-Aware Quantization (DAQ), a data-free post-training quantization framework that preserves the knowledge acquired during post-training. Standard quantization objectives minimize reconstruction error but are agnostic to the base model, allowing quantization noise to disproportionately corrupt the small-magnitude parameter deltas ($ΔW$) that encode post-training behavior -- an effect we analyze through the lens of quantization as implicit regularization. DAQ replaces reconstruction-based objectives with two delta-aware metrics -- Sign Preservation Rate and Cosine Similarity -- that directly optimize for directional fidelity of $ΔW$, requiring only the base and post-trained weight matrices. In a pilot FP8 study, DAQ recovers style-specific capabilities lost under standard quantization while maintaining general performance.

</details>


### [39] [Hybrid Associative Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22325)
*Leon Lufkin,Tomás Figliolia,Beren Millidge,Kamesh Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HAM层结合RNN和自注意力机制，利用RNN压缩整个序列，而注意力仅补充RNN难以预测的最有价值信息，实现KV缓存的数据依赖增长和用户可控调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN和自注意力机制在序列建模中各有优缺点：RNN将整个过去压缩为固定大小状态，效率高但长上下文性能下降；自注意力存储每个过去时间步，KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，检索能力强但计算内存成本高。现有工作简单交错这两种机制以降低计算成本，未充分利用其互补特性。

Method: 提出混合关联记忆（HAM）层，结合RNN和自注意力机制：RNN压缩整个序列，注意力仅补充RNN难以预测的最有价值信息。通过单一连续阈值实现用户对KV缓存增长率的精确控制，实现数据依赖的KV缓存增长。

Result: HAM层在显著降低KV缓存使用量的情况下，相对于RNN和Transformer表现出强大且具有竞争力的性能。KV缓存增长率与损失/性能之间存在平滑的权衡关系，用户可通过阈值进行精细控制。

Conclusion: HAM层通过有机结合RNN和自注意力机制的互补优势，实现了高效且高性能的序列建模。该方法不仅降低了计算和内存成本，还提供了用户可控的KV缓存管理机制，为序列建模架构设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recurrent neural networks (RNNs) and self-attention are both widely used sequence-mixing layers that maintain an internal memory. However, this memory is constructed using two orthogonal mechanisms: RNNs compress the entire past into a fixed-size state, whereas self-attention's state stores every past time step growing its state (the KV cache) linearly with the sequence length. This results in orthogonal strengths and weaknesses. Self-attention layers excel at retrieving information in the context but have large memory and computational costs, while RNNs are more efficient but degrade over longer contexts and underperform for precise recall tasks. Prior work combining these mechanisms has focused primarily on naively interleaving them to reduce computational cost without regard to their complementary mechanisms. We propose the Hybrid Associative Memory (HAM) layer, which combines self-attention and RNNs while leveraging their individual strengths: the RNN compresses the entire sequence, while attention supplements it *only* with information that is difficult for the RNN to predict, which is hence the most valuable information to explicitly store. HAM layers enable data-dependent growth of the KV cache, which can be precisely controlled by the user with a single, continuous threshold. We find that this fine-grained control of the KV cache growth rate has a smooth trade-off with loss and performance. Empirically, we show that our hybrid architecture offers strong, competitive performance relative to RNNs and Transformers even at substantially lower KV-cache usage.

</details>


### [40] [A Direct Classification Approach for Reliable Wind Ramp Event Forecasting under Severe Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22326)
*Alejandro Morales-Hernández,Fabrizio De Caroa,Gian Marco Paldino,Pascal Tribel,Alfredo Vaccaro,Gianluca Bontempi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对风电功率爬坡事件预测的新方法，通过多数类欠采样和集成学习解决类别不平衡问题，在真实数据集上取得了超过85%准确率和88%加权F1分数的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低碳电力系统中，决策支持系统对维持电网稳定至关重要，需要为控制室操作员提供风电功率爬坡事件的实时预警。然而，由于WPRE数据集中爬坡事件较少（通常低于15%），存在严重的类别不平衡问题，传统机器学习模型往往偏向多数类，导致预测性能下降。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的WPRE预测方法，将其视为多元时间序列分类任务。采用数据预处理策略从近期功率观测中提取特征，并屏蔽不可用的爬坡信息，使其能够与传统实时爬坡识别工具集成。特别地，该方法结合了多数类欠采样和集成学习技术来增强类别不平衡条件下的风电爬坡事件预测能力。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的数值模拟表明，该方法表现出优越性能，实现了超过85%的准确率和88%的加权F1分数，优于基准分类器。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的结合多数类欠采样和集成学习的方法有效解决了风电功率爬坡事件预测中的类别不平衡问题，为低碳电力系统的电网稳定提供了有效的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Decision support systems are essential for maintaining grid stability in low-carbon power systems, such as wind power plants, by providing real-time alerts to control room operators regarding potential events, including Wind Power Ramp Events (WPREs). These early warnings enable the timely initiation of more detailed system stability assessments and preventive actions. However, forecasting these events is challenging due to the inherent class imbalance in WPRE datasets, where ramp events are less frequent (typically less than 15\% of observed events) compared to normal conditions. Ignoring this characteristic undermines the performance of conventional machine learning models, which often favor the majority class. This paper introduces a novel methodology for WPRE forecasting as a multivariate time series classification task and proposes a data preprocessing strategy that extracts features from recent power observations and masks unavailable ramp information, making it integrable with traditional real-time ramp identification tools. Particularly, the proposed methodology combines majority-class undersampling and ensemble learning to enhance wind ramp event forecasting under class imbalance. Numerical simulations conducted on a real-world dataset demonstrate the superiority of our approach, achieving over 85% accuracy and 88% weighted F1 score, outperforming benchmark classifiers.

</details>


### [41] [Trained Persistent Memory for Frozen Decoder-Only LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22329)
*Hong Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将记忆适配器从编码器-解码器架构迁移到仅解码器架构，研究在缺乏交叉注意力路径的情况下，通过自注意力实现持久潜在空间记忆的可行性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅解码器语言模型是无状态的，隐藏表示在前向传播后被丢弃，会话间无持久性。先前研究在编码器-解码器架构中通过记忆适配器实现了持久潜在空间记忆，本研究探索该原理是否适用于仅解码器架构，其中没有交叉注意力路径，记忆必须仅通过自注意力注入

Method: 将六种记忆方法（前缀、并行交叉注意力、KV扩展、Hebbian记忆、上下文门控分支、基于槽的稀疏写入）适配到冻结的GPT-2模型，仅训练小型记忆适配器θ_mem。写入规则共享，读取注入从解码器交叉注意力改为自注意力KV前缀或并行分支。在LoCoMo基准上进行评估

Result: 在1倍容量下发现显著的归纳偏置二分：具有强架构先验的三种方法（交叉注意力M.2、Hebbian M.4、槽写入M.6）获得7-18%的保留记忆分数和7-10的ΔK知识增益，而其他三种方法失败（<0.4%）。在10倍容量下所有六种方法收敛，表明差距是架构性的而非根本性的

Conclusion: 结合Jeong (2026a)的编码器-解码器结果和Jeong (2026b,c)的脑启发模块，这些发现确立了持久潜在空间记忆作为一个通用范式，跨越主要Transformer家族

Abstract: Decoder-only language models are stateless: hidden representations are discarded after every forward pass and nothing persists across sessions.
