SpecXMaster enables automated extraction of multiplicity information from both 1H and 13C spectra directly from raw FID (free induction decay) data. This end-to-end pipeline enables fully automated interpretation of NMR spectra into chemical structures. It demonstrates superior performance across multiple public NMR interpretation benchmarks and has been refined through iterative evaluations by professional chemical spectroscopists. We believe that SpecXMaster, as a novel methodological paradigm for spectral interpretation, will have a profound impact on the organic chemistry community.

</details>


### [77] [DAK-UCB: Diversity-Aware Prompt Routing for LLMs and Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23140)
*Donya Jafari,Farzan Farnia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DAK-UCB方法，一种考虑多样性的上下文赌博机算法，用于在线选择生成模型，平衡保真度和多样性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于保真度的生成模型选择方法（如CLIP-Score）忽视了生成输出的多样性，可能导致生成响应缺乏多样性

Method: 提出多样性感知核化上置信界（DAK-UCB）方法，将保真度和多样性指标结合到选择过程中，基于提示感知的多样性评分函数，使用联合核距离和核熵度量

Result: 实验结果表明DAK-UCB在保持生成保真度的同时，有效促进了多样性感知的模型选择

Conclusion: DAK-UCB框架成功解决了生成模型选择中多样性不足的问题，为在线模型选择提供了兼顾保真度和多样性的有效方法

Abstract: The expansion of generative AI and LLM services underscores the growing need for adaptive mechanisms to select an appropriate available model to respond to a user's prompts. Recent works have proposed offline and online learning formulations to identify the optimal generative AI model for an input prompt, based solely on maximizing prompt-based fidelity evaluation scores, e.g., CLIP-Score in text-to-image generation. However, such fidelity-based selection methods overlook the diversity of generated outputs, and hence, they can fail to address potential diversity shortcomings in the generated responses. In this paper, we introduce the Diversity-Aware Kernelized Upper Confidence Bound (DAK-UCB) method as a contextual bandit algorithm for the online selection of generative models with diversity considerations. The proposed DAK-UCB method incorporates both fidelity and diversity-related metrics into the selection process. We design this framework based on prompt-aware diversity score functions that decompose to a two-sample-based expectation over prompt-output pairs in the previous generation rounds. Specifically, we illustrate the application of our framework using joint kernel distance and kernel entropy measures. Our experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of DAK-UCB in promoting diversity-aware model selection while maintaining fidelity in the generations for a sequence of prompts. The code is available at https://github.com/Donya-Jafari/DAK-UCB.

</details>


### [78] [Sparser, Faster, Lighter Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23198)
*Edoardo Cetin,Stefano Peluchetti,Emilio Castillo,Akira Naruse,Mana Murakami,Llion Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种通过LLM前馈层稀疏化来降低计算成本的方法，包括新的稀疏打包格式和CUDA内核，结合L1正则化实现>99%稀疏度且性能损失可忽略，显著提升推理/训练吞吐量、能效和内存使用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型扩展带来巨大计算成本，特别是前馈层占用了大部分参数和计算FLOPs。通过利用前馈层的非结构化稀疏性来降低这些成本。

Method: 1) 引入新的稀疏打包格式；2) 设计一组CUDA内核，与现代GPU优化执行流水线无缝集成；3) 使用简单的L1正则化诱导超过99%的稀疏度；4) 提供LLM稀疏性的定量研究。

Result: L1正则化可诱导超过99%的稀疏度且对下游性能影响可忽略。结合提出的内核，这些稀疏度水平转化为显著的吞吐量提升、能效改善和内存使用效益，且效益随模型规模增大而增加。

Conclusion: 通过稀疏化前馈层和优化的计算内核，为现代基础模型建立了稀疏性作为提高效率和可扩展性的实用维度，所有代码和内核将开源以促进采用和加速研究。

Abstract: Scaling autoregressive large language models (LLMs) has driven unprecedented progress but comes with vast computational costs. In this work, we tackle these costs by leveraging unstructured sparsity within an LLM's feedforward layers, the components accounting for most of the model parameters and execution FLOPs. To achieve this, we introduce a new sparse packing format and a set of CUDA kernels designed to seamlessly integrate with the optimized execution pipelines of modern GPUs, enabling efficient sparse computation during LLM inference and training. To substantiate our gains, we provide a quantitative study of LLM sparsity, demonstrating that simple L1 regularization can induce over 99% sparsity with negligible impact on downstream performance. When paired with our kernels, we show that these sparsity levels translate into substantial throughput, energy efficiency, and memory usage benefits that increase with model scale. We will release all code and kernels under an open-source license to promote adoption and accelerate research toward establishing sparsity as a practical axis for improving the efficiency and scalability of modern foundation models.

</details>


### [79] [A One-Inclusion Graph Approach to Multi-Group Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23208)
*Noah Bergam,Samuel Deng,Daniel Hsu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了多组学习样本复杂度的最紧上界，通过扩展单包含图预测策略并使用二分图b-匹配的泛化方法，在组可实现设置下证明了log n/n收敛率的最优性，并在评估组与样本无关的松弛目标下达到1/n最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多组学习的样本复杂度，旨在找到更紧的上界并设计最优算法，解决现有方法在收敛率上的局限性，特别是在组可实现设置下的最优收敛问题。

Method: 扩展单包含图预测策略，引入二分图b-匹配的泛化方法，设计新的多组学习算法，分析其在组可实现设置下的收敛性能。

Result: 证明了多组学习样本复杂度的最紧上界，算法在组可实现设置下达到log n/n收敛率且被证明是最优的；在评估组与样本无关的松弛目标下，算法能达到1/n的最优收敛率。

Conclusion: 提出的算法在多组学习中实现了最优收敛性能，在组可实现设置下log n/n收敛率是最优的，在松弛目标下能达到1/n的最优收敛率，为多组学习提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We prove the tightest-known upper bounds on the sample complexity of multi-group learning. Our algorithm extends the one-inclusion graph prediction strategy using a generalization of bipartite $b$-matching. In the group-realizable setting, we provide a lower bound confirming that our algorithm's $\log n / n$ convergence rate is optimal in general. If one relaxes the learning objective such that the group on which we are evaluated is chosen obliviously of the sample, then our algorithm achieves the optimal $1/n$ convergence rate under group-realizability.

</details>


### [80] [General Machine Learning: Theory for Learning Under Variable Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23220)
*Aomar Osmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个学习理论框架，用于处理学习过程中学习者、记忆状态和评估条件可能随时间变化的"制度变化"场景，建立了核心概念和初步定理结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在制度变化下的学习问题，即学习者、其记忆状态以及评估条件可能随时间演变的情况。目标是建立适用于此类场景的核心学习理论对象，并确立其首个定理支持的结果。

Method: 开发了一个以可容许传输、保护核心保持和评估者感知学习演化为中心的制度变化框架。建立了可容许性的直接闭包结果，为真正多制度设置中的忠实固定本体论约简开发了结构阻碍论证，并引入了保护稳定性模板及其在受控子类上的显式数值和符号见证。

Result: 建立了关于评估者分解、态射、组合以及语义可比较层之间的部分核级对齐的定理层结果。通过一个两制度的工作示例，在受控子类上明确了可容许性证书、保护评估核心和制度变化成本。获得了首个核级兼容性结果和一个受控单调演绎见证。

Conclusion: 该论文为制度变化学习引入了一个结构化学习理论框架及其首个定理支持层，而不是一个完整的所有学习系统的定量理论。符号组件被有意限制在有限范围内，旨在建立基础结构而非全面定量分析。

Abstract: We study learning under regime variation, where the learner, its memory state, and the evaluative conditions may evolve over time. This paper is a foundational and structural contribution: its goal is to define the core learning-theoretic objects required for such settings and to establish their first theorem-supporting consequences.
  The paper develops a regime-varying framework centered on admissible transport, protected-core preservation, and evaluator-aware learning evolution. It records the immediate closure consequences of admissibility, develops a structural obstruction argument for faithful fixed-ontology reduction in genuinely multi-regime settings, and introduces a protected-stability template together with explicit numerical and symbolic witnesses on controlled subclasses, including convex and deductive settings. It also establishes theorem-layer results on evaluator factorization, morphisms, composition, and partial kernel-level alignment across semantically commensurable layers.
  A worked two-regime example makes the admissibility certificate, protected evaluative core, and regime-variation cost explicit on a controlled subclass. The symbolic component is deliberately restricted in scope: the paper establishes a first kernel-level compatibility result together with a controlled monotonic deductive witness. The manuscript should therefore be read as introducing a structured learning-theoretic framework for regime-varying learning together with its first theorem-supporting layer, not as a complete quantitative theory of all learning systems.

</details>


### [81] [GEM: Guided Expectation-Maximization for Behavior-Normalized Candidate Action Selection in Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23232)
*Haoyu Wang,Jingcheng Wang,Shunyu Wu,Xinwei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM是一个用于离线强化学习的多模态动作选择框架，通过高斯混合模型演员和保守的候选重排序机制，解决数据集诱导的多模态动作分布问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习虽然能从固定数据集中拟合强大的价值函数，但当数据集诱导出分支或多模态的动作分布时，单模态的策略提取会模糊竞争假设，产生数据支持弱的"中间"动作，即使有强大的评论家也会导致决策脆弱。

Method: GEM框架包含：1) 通过评论家引导、优势加权的EM风格更新训练高斯混合模型演员，保持不同组件同时将概率质量移向高价值区域；2) 学习可处理的高斯混合模型行为模型以量化支持度；3) 推理时进行基于候选的选择：生成并行候选集，使用保守的集成下置信界和行为归一化支持度重排序动作。

Result: GEM在D4RL基准测试中具有竞争力，提供了一个简单的推理时预算旋钮（候选数量），可以在不重新训练的情况下用计算换取决策质量。

Conclusion: GEM通过多模态、显式可控的动作选择框架，解决了离线强化学习中数据集诱导的多模态动作分布问题，提供了稳定、可比较的控制，并允许在推理时灵活调整计算预算。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) can fit strong value functions from fixed datasets, yet reliable deployment still hinges on the action selection interface used to query them. When the dataset induces a branched or multimodal action landscape, unimodal policy extraction can blur competing hypotheses and yield "in-between" actions that are weakly supported by data, making decisions brittle even with a strong critic. We introduce GEM (Guided Expectation-Maximization), an analytical framework that makes action selection both multimodal and explicitly controllable. GEM trains a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) actor via critic-guided, advantage-weighted EM-style updates that preserve distinct components while shifting probability mass toward high-value regions, and learns a tractable GMM behavior model to quantify support. During inference, GEM performs candidate-based selection: it generates a parallel candidate set and reranks actions using a conservative ensemble lower-confidence bound together with behavior-normalized support, where the behavior log-likelihood is standardized within each state's candidate set to yield stable, comparable control across states and candidate budgets. Empirically, GEM is competitive across D4RL benchmarks, and offers a simple inference-time budget knob (candidate count) that trades compute for decision quality without retraining.

</details>


### [82] [Neural ODE and SDE Models for Adaptation and Planning in Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23245)
*Chao Han,Stefanos Ioannou,Luca Manneschi,T. J. Hayward,Michael Mangan,Aditya Gilra,Eleni Vasilaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探索了神经ODE和SDE在模型强化学习框架中建模随机动态，证明神经SDE能更有效捕捉环境随机性，并开发了用于部分可观测环境的潜在SDE模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型强化学习中，准确建模环境的随机动态对于策略学习和适应至关重要。传统方法在处理完全和部分可观测环境中的随机过渡动态时面临挑战，需要更有效的随机动态建模方法。

Method: 研究采用神经ODE和SDE来建模随机动态：1) 在完全可观测环境中使用神经SDE捕捉过渡动态的固有随机性；2) 通过逆模型实现高效策略适应；3) 针对部分可观测性，提出潜在SDE模型，将ODE与GAN训练的随机组件结合在潜在空间中。

Result: 神经SDE能更有效地捕捉过渡动态的随机性，在挑战性场景中实现高性能策略并提高样本效率。潜在SDE模型在随机连续控制基准测试中优于或匹配一般基于模型和无模型方法，为部分可观测环境提供了强大基线。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了动作条件潜在SDE在具有随机过渡的环境中进行强化学习规划的有效性，为处理环境随机性和部分可观测性提供了新的建模框架。

Abstract: We investigate neural ordinary and stochastic differential equations (neural ODEs and SDEs) to model stochastic dynamics in fully and partially observed environments within a model-based reinforcement learning (RL) framework. Through a sequence of simulations, we show that neural SDEs more effectively capture the inherent stochasticity of transition dynamics, enabling high-performing policies with improved sample efficiency in challenging scenarios. We leverage neural ODEs and SDEs for efficient policy adaptation to changes in environment dynamics via inverse models, requiring only limited interactions with the new environment. To address partial observability, we introduce a latent SDE model that combines an ODE with a GAN-trained stochastic component in latent space. Policies derived from this model provide a strong baseline, outperforming or matching general model-based and model-free approaches across stochastic continuous-control benchmarks. This work demonstrates the applicability of action-conditional latent SDEs for RL planning in environments with stochastic transitions. Our code is available at: https://github.com/ChaoHan-UoS/NeuralRL

</details>


### [83] [A Learning Method with Gap-Aware Generation for Heterogeneous DAG Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23249)
*Ruisong Zhou,Haijun Zou,Li Zhou,Chumin Sun,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeCAN：一种用于异构DAG调度的端到端强化学习框架，采用两阶段单次前向设计，通过加权交叉注意力编码器建模任务-资源池交互，并引入跳转扩展机制消除生成诱导的最优性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构环境中DAG调度面临资源容量和依赖关系的挑战，需要适应不同资源池和任务类型的环境变化，同时要求快速生成调度方案。现有列表调度方法存在生成诱导的最优性差距问题。

Method: 提出WeCAN框架：1）采用两阶段单次前向设计，一次前向传播生成任务-资源池分数和全局参数，然后通过生成映射构建调度；2）使用加权交叉注意力编码器建模任务-资源池交互，通过兼容性系数门控；3）引入顺序空间分析，表征生成映射的可达调度顺序集；4）设计跳转扩展实现，采用分析参数化的递减跳转规则扩大可达顺序集。

Result: 在计算图和真实世界TPC-H DAG上的实验表明，WeCAN相比强基线改进了完工时间，推理时间与经典启发式算法相当，且比多轮神经调度器更快。

Conclusion: WeCAN通过端到端强化学习框架有效解决了异构DAG调度问题，其单次前向设计和跳转扩展机制在保持效率的同时提高了调度质量，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient scheduling of directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) in heterogeneous environments is challenging due to resource capacities and dependencies. In practice, the need for adaptability across environments with varying resource pools and task types, alongside rapid schedule generation, complicates these challenges. We propose WeCAN, an end-to-end reinforcement learning framework for heterogeneous DAG scheduling that addresses task--pool compatibility coefficients and generation-induced optimality gaps. It adopts a two-stage single-pass design: a single forward pass produces task--pool scores and global parameters, followed by a generation map that constructs schedules without repeated network calls. Its weighted cross-attention encoder models task--pool interactions gated by compatibility coefficients, and is size-agnostic to environment fluctuations. Moreover, widely used list-scheduling maps can incur generation-induced optimality gaps from restricted reachability. We introduce an order-space analysis that characterizes the reachable set of generation maps via feasible schedule orders, explains the mechanism behind generation-induced gaps, and yields sufficient conditions for gap elimination. Guided by these conditions, we design a skip-extended realization with an analytically parameterized decreasing skip rule, which enlarges the reachable order set while preserving single-pass efficiency. Experiments on computation graphs and real-world TPC-H DAGs demonstrate improved makespan over strong baselines, with inference time comparable to classical heuristics and faster than multi-round neural schedulers.

</details>


### [84] [SafeSeek: Universal Attribution of Safety Circuits in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23268)
*Miao Yu,Siyuan Fu,Moayad Aloqaily,Zhenhong Zhou,Safa Otoum,Xing fan,Kun Wang,Yufei Guo,Qingsong Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的LLM安全可解释性框架，通过优化方法识别功能完整的安全电路，并在后门攻击和安全对齐两个关键场景中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全归因方法依赖启发式、领域特定的度量和搜索算法，导致泛化性和可靠性不足。需要一种统一框架来识别LLM中的功能完整安全电路。

Method: 提出\ourmethod框架，引入可微分二元掩码，通过梯度下降在安全数据集上提取多粒度电路，并集成安全电路调优，利用稀疏电路进行高效安全微调。

Result: 在后门攻击场景中识别出稀疏度为0.42%的后门电路，其消融使攻击成功率从100%降至0.4%，同时保持99%以上通用效用；在安全对齐场景中定位到包含3.03%头部和0.79%神经元的对齐电路，其移除使攻击成功率从0.8%飙升至96.9%，而在有帮助性微调中排除该电路可保持96.5%的安全保留率。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种统一、优化的方法来识别LLM中的功能完整安全电路，在安全归因和高效安全微调方面展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability reveals that safety-critical behaviors (e.g., alignment, jailbreak, backdoor) in Large Language Models (LLMs) are grounded in specialized functional components. However, existing safety attribution methods struggle with generalization and reliability due to their reliance on heuristic, domain-specific metrics and search algorithms. To address this, we propose \ourmethod, a unified safety interpretability framework that identifies functionally complete safety circuits in LLMs via optimization. Unlike methods focusing on isolated heads or neurons, \ourmethod introduces differentiable binary masks to extract multi-granular circuits through gradient descent on safety datasets, while integrates Safety Circuit Tuning to utilize these sparse circuits for efficient safety fine-tuning. We validate \ourmethod in two key scenarios in LLM safety: \textbf{(1) backdoor attacks}, identifying a backdoor circuit with 0.42\% sparsity, whose ablation eradicates the Attack Success Rate (ASR) from 100\% $\to$ 0.4\% while retaining over 99\% general utility; \textbf{(2) safety alignment}, localizing an alignment circuit with 3.03\% heads and 0.79\% neurons, whose removal spikes ASR from 0.8\% $\to$ 96.9\%, whereas excluding this circuit during helpfulness fine-tuning maintains 96.5\% safety retention.

</details>


### [85] [A Comparative Study of Machine Learning Models for Hourly Forecasting of Air Temperature and Relative Humidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23282)
*Jiaqi Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究比较了七种机器学习模型在重庆复杂地形城市中空气温度和相对湿度小时预测的性能，发现XGBoost在准确性和鲁棒性方面表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测空气温度和相对湿度对复杂地形城市管理至关重要，特别是在重庆这样的山地城市。需要评估不同机器学习模型在气象时间序列预测中的性能，为智能气象预报提供实践指导。

Method: 采用统一的数据预处理框架，包括滞后特征构建、滚动统计特征和时间序列验证。比较了七种机器学习模型：XGBoost、随机森林、支持向量回归、多层感知器、决策树、LSTM和CNN-LSTM，使用真实世界开放数据进行小时预测。

Result: XGBoost在整体性能上表现最佳，空气温度测试MAE为0.302°C，相对湿度MAE为1.271%，两个预测任务的平均R²为0.989。树基集成学习方法在结构化气象时间序列预测中表现出强大效果。

Conclusion: 树基集成学习（特别是XGBoost）在复杂地形城市气象预测中表现优异，为山地城市的智能气象预报提供了实用指导。该研究证明了机器学习模型在结构化时间序列预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate short-term forecasting of air temperature and relative humidity is critical for urban management, especially in topographically complex cities such as Chongqing, China. This study compares seven machine learning models: eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), Random Forest, Support Vector Regression (SVR), Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP), Decision Tree, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and Convolutional Neural Network (CNN)-LSTM (CNN-LSTM), for hourly prediction using real-world open data. Based on a unified framework of data preprocessing, lag-feature construction, rolling statistical features, and time-series validation, the models are systematically evaluated in terms of predictive accuracy and robustness. The results show that XGBoost achieves the best overall performance, with a test mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.302 °C for air temperature and 1.271% for relative humidity, together with an average R2 of 0.989 across the two forecasting tasks. These findings demonstrate the strong effectiveness of tree-based ensemble learning for structured meteorological time-series forecasting and provide practical guidance for intelligent meteorological forecasting in mountainous cities.

</details>


### [86] [Robustness Quantification for Discriminative Models: a New Robustness Metric and its Application to Dynamic Classifier Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23318)
*Rodrigo F. L. Lassance,Jasper De Bock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出适用于任何概率判别分类器和任何特征类型的新鲁棒性度量，用于评估预测可靠性并开发动态分类器选择策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒性量化方法存在局限性：需要生成模型、仅限于特定模型架构或离散特征，限制了其适用性

Method: 提出新的鲁棒性度量方法，适用于任何概率判别分类器和任何类型特征，基于该度量开发动态分类器选择策略

Result: 新度量能够有效区分可靠与不可靠预测，为动态分类器选择提供了新方法

Conclusion: 提出的鲁棒性度量克服了现有方法的局限性，为评估分类器预测可靠性和动态选择提供了通用框架

Abstract: Among the different possible strategies for evaluating the reliability of individual predictions of classifiers, robustness quantification stands out as a method that evaluates how much uncertainty a classifier could cope with before changing its prediction. However, its applicability is more limited than some of its alternatives, since it requires the use of generative models and restricts the analyses either to specific model architectures or discrete features. In this work, we propose a new robustness metric applicable to any probabilistic discriminative classifier and any type of features. We demonstrate that this new metric is capable of distinguishing between reliable and unreliable predictions, and use this observation to develop new strategies for dynamic classifier selection.

</details>


### [87] [Off-Policy Value-Based Reinforcement Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23355)
*Peng-Yuan Wang,Ziniu Li,Tian Xu,Bohan Yang,Tian-Shuo Liu,ChenYang Wang,Xiong-Hui Chen,Yi-Chen Li,Tianyun Yang,Congliang Chen,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReVal：一种基于贝尔曼更新的价值强化学习方法，通过结合内部一致性的逐步信号和结果验证的轨迹级信号，实现LLM的高效离策略学习，显著提升训练效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM强化学习方法主要是同策略的，每批数据只使用一次就被丢弃，导致样本效率低下。对于生成轨迹成本高昂的长时程任务，提高数据利用效率至关重要。

Method: 提出ReVal方法，基于价值强化学习框架，结合贝尔曼更新。该方法整合了捕捉内部一致性的逐步信号和基于结果验证的轨迹级信号，支持基于回放缓冲区的训练，能够高效重用历史轨迹。

Result: 在标准数学推理基准测试中，ReVal不仅收敛更快，最终性能也优于GRPO。在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-1.5B模型上，ReVal提高了训练效率，在AIME24上提升2.7%，在GPQA上提升4.5%。

Conclusion: 基于价值的强化学习是LLM训练中实用且高效的替代方案，相比策略方法具有更好的样本效率和性能表现。

Abstract: Improving data utilization efficiency is critical for scaling reinforcement learning (RL) for long-horizon tasks where generating trajectories is expensive. However, the dominant RL methods for LLMs are largely on-policy: they update each batch of data only once, discard it, and then collect fresh samples, resulting in poor sample efficiency. In this work, we explore an alternative value-based RL framework for LLMs that naturally enables off-policy learning. We propose ReVal, a Bellman-update-based method that combines stepwise signals capturing internal consistency with trajectory-level signals derived from outcome verification. ReVal naturally supports replay-buffer-based training, allowing efficient reuse of past trajectories. Experiments on standard mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that ReVal not only converges faster but also outperforms GRPO in final performance. On DeepSeek-R1-Distill-1.5B, ReVal improves training efficiency and achieves improvement of 2.7% in AIME24 and 4.5% in out-of-domain benchmark GPQA over GRPO. These results suggest that value-based RL is a practical alternative to policy-based methods for LLM training.

</details>


### [88] [Central Dogma Transformer III: Interpretable AI Across DNA, RNA, and Protein](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23361)
*Nobuyuki Ota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CDT-III是一个机制导向的AI模型，通过虚拟细胞嵌入器架构模拟中心法则全过程，实现DNA到RNA再到蛋白质的预测，并利用下游任务正则化上游表示，提高可解释性和临床应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生物AI模型虽然能预测复杂的细胞响应，但其学习到的表征与它们试图捕捉的分子过程之间存在脱节。需要开发能够连接中心法则各阶段（DNA、RNA、蛋白质）的机制导向AI模型，以更好地理解和预测细胞功能。

Method: 提出CDT-III模型，采用两阶段虚拟细胞嵌入器架构，模拟细胞空间区室化：VCE-N在细胞核中建模转录过程，VCE-C在细胞质中建模翻译过程。模型通过蛋白质预测任务来正则化RNA表示，利用梯度分析进行副作用分析。

Result: 在5个保留基因上，CDT-III实现RNA预测相关系数r=0.843，蛋白质预测r=0.969。添加蛋白质预测任务将RNA性能从r=0.804提升到0.843。蛋白质监督使DNA层面可解释性提高，CTCF富集增加30%。在模拟CD52敲除实验中，正确预测29/29个蛋白质变化，重新发现5/7已知临床副作用，无需临床数据。基于梯度的副作用分析仅需未扰动基线数据（r=0.939），可筛选所有2,361个基因。

Conclusion: CDT-III成功将机制导向AI扩展到整个中心法则，通过下游任务正则化上游表示提高了模型性能和可解释性。该框架能够仅使用基线数据预测基因扰动效应，为药物副作用筛查和机制理解提供了强大的计算工具。

Abstract: Biological AI models increasingly predict complex cellular responses, yet their learned representations remain disconnected from the molecular processes they aim to capture. We present CDT-III, which extends mechanism-oriented AI across the full central dogma: DNA, RNA, and protein. Its two-stage Virtual Cell Embedder architecture mirrors the spatial compartmentalization of the cell: VCE-N models transcription in the nucleus and VCE-C models translation in the cytosol. On five held-out genes, CDT-III achieves per-gene RNA r=0.843 and protein r=0.969. Adding protein prediction improves RNA performance (r=0.804 to 0.843), demonstrating that downstream tasks regularize upstream representations. Protein supervision sharpens DNA-level interpretability, increasing CTCF enrichment by 30%. Applied to in silico CD52 knockdown approximating Alemtuzumab, the model predicts 29/29 protein changes correctly and rediscovers 5 of 7 known clinical side effects without clinical data. Gradient-based side effect profiling requires only unperturbed baseline data (r=0.939), enabling screening of all 2,361 genes without new experiments.

</details>


### [89] [Graph Energy Matching: Transport-Aligned Energy-Based Modeling for Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23398)
*Michal Balcerak,Suprosana Shit,Chinmay Prabhakar,Sebastian Kaltenbach,Michael S. Albergo,Yilun Du,Bjoern Menze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM（Graph Energy Matching）是一种用于图生成的基于能量的模型框架，通过JKO方案的传输映射优化视角，学习排列不变势能，结合梯度引导传输和混合探索机制，在分子图基准上达到或超过离散扩散模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散能量模型（如图）能显式捕获相对似然，支持可组合的概率推理任务（如条件生成或约束强制执行），但传统上存在采样效率和质量问题，采样器易陷入伪局部最小值，导致训练不稳定，与离散扩散模型存在保真度差距。

Method: GEM基于Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto（JKO）方案的传输映射优化视角，学习排列不变势能，该势能同时提供从噪声到数据的传输对齐引导，并在高数据似然区域内细化样本。引入基于能量的切换采样协议，无缝桥接：（i）快速梯度引导传输到高概率区域和（ii）混合探索机制以探索学习到的图分布。

Result: 在分子图基准测试中，GEM匹配或超过了强大的离散扩散基线模型。除了样本质量外，显式相对似然建模支持推理时的针对性探索，促进了组合生成、属性约束采样和图之间的测地线插值。

Conclusion: GEM框架成功弥合了离散能量模型与扩散模型之间的保真度差距，通过传输对齐的势能学习和混合采样协议，实现了高效高质量图生成，同时保留了能量模型在可组合推理和约束执行方面的优势。

Abstract: Energy-based models for discrete domains, such as graphs, explicitly capture relative likelihoods, naturally enabling composable probabilistic inference tasks like conditional generation or enforcing constraints at test-time. However, discrete energy-based models typically struggle with efficient and high-quality sampling, as off-support regions often contain spurious local minima, trapping samplers and causing training instabilities. This has historically resulted in a fidelity gap relative to discrete diffusion models. We introduce Graph Energy Matching (GEM), a generative framework for graphs that closes this fidelity gap. Motivated by the transport map optimization perspective of the Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) scheme, GEM learns a permutation-invariant potential energy that simultaneously provides transport-aligned guidance from noise toward data and refines samples within regions of high data likelihood. Further, we introduce a sampling protocol that leverages an energy-based switch to seamlessly bridge: (i) rapid, gradient-guided transport toward high-probability regions to (ii) a mixing regime for exploration of the learned graph distribution. On molecular graph benchmarks, GEM matches or exceeds strong discrete diffusion baselines. Beyond sample quality, explicit modeling of relative likelihood enables targeted exploration at inference time, facilitating compositional generation, property-constrained sampling, and geodesic interpolation between graphs.

</details>


### [90] [SortedRL: Accelerating RL Training for LLMs through Online Length-Aware Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23414)
*Yiqi Zhang,Huiqiang Jiang,Xufang Luo,Zhihe Yang,Chengruidong Zhang,Yifei Shen,Dongsheng Li,Yuqing Yang,Lili Qiu,Yang You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SortedRL通过基于输出长度的在线调度策略，优化RL训练中的rollout效率瓶颈，减少50%以上训练时间，同时提升模型性能3.9%-18.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在提升大语言模型推理能力方面表现出潜力，但训练效率受限于rollout阶段，该阶段在生成长轨迹（如16k tokens）时可占总训练时间的70%，主要由于自回归生成速度慢以及rollout与策略更新间的同步开销。

Method: 提出SortedRL：基于输出长度的在线调度策略，通过按长度重新排序rollout样本，优先处理短样本形成组进行早期更新，实现大rollout批次、灵活更新批次和近在线微课程构建。结合缓存机制控制离策略训练程度，并配备专用RL基础设施（状态控制器和rollout缓冲区）。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Qwen-2.5-32B上的实验表明，SortedRL将RL训练气泡比降低超过50%，在相同数据量下性能比基线提升3.9%到18.4%。测试任务包括逻辑谜题和数学挑战（AIME 24、Math 500、Minerval）。

Conclusion: SortedRL有效解决了RL训练中的rollout效率瓶颈，通过智能调度显著加速训练过程，同时保持或提升模型性能，为大语言模型的强化学习训练提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Scaling reinforcement learning (RL) has shown strong promise for enhancing the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs), particularly in tasks requiring long chain-of-thought generation. However, RL training efficiency is often bottlenecked by the rollout phase, which can account for up to 70% of total training time when generating long trajectories (e.g., 16k tokens), due to slow autoregressive generation and synchronization overhead between rollout and policy updates. We propose SortedRL, an online length-aware scheduling strategy designed to address this bottleneck by improving rollout efficiency and maintaining training stability. SortedRL reorders rollout samples based on output lengths, prioritizing short samples forming groups for early updates. This enables large rollout batches, flexible update batches, and near on-policy micro-curriculum construction simultaneously. To further accelerate the pipeline, SortedRL incorporates a mechanism to control the degree of off-policy training through a cache-based mechanism, and is supported by a dedicated RL infrastructure that manages rollout and update via a stateful controller and rollout buffer. Experiments using LLaMA-3.1-8B and Qwen-2.5-32B on diverse tasks, including logical puzzles, and math challenges like AIME 24, Math 500, and Minerval, show that SortedRL reduces RL training bubble ratios by over 50%, while attaining 3.9% to 18.4% superior performance over baseline given same amount of data.

</details>


### [91] [Similarity-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Data-Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23436)
*Connor Mclaughlin,Nigel Lee,Lili Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于预训练模型的自适应专家混合框架，用于解决任务重叠且数据稀缺的持续学习问题，通过增量全局池化和实例级提示掩码来提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法通常假设每个任务有足够数据样本或任务间无重叠，但实际应用中任务可能数据有限且以任意方式重叠，这带来两个挑战：数据稀缺需要有效知识迁移，而任务重叠可能导致负向知识迁移。

Method: 提出自适应专家混合框架，包含两个创新算法组件：1) 增量全局池化：随时间逐步引入提示以减少提示关联噪声；2) 实例级提示掩码：将任务样本分解为与当前提示对齐的分布内样本和需要新提示的分布外样本。

Result: 在不同数据量和任务间相似度的实验中，该方法提高了样本效率并具有广泛适用性，能够有效利用任务重叠潜力同时防止负向相互干扰。

Conclusion: 该自适应专家混合框架成功解决了任务重叠且数据稀缺的持续学习挑战，通过渐进建立任务间相似性感知，在数据有限条件下实现了有效的知识迁移和负向干扰预防。

Abstract: Machine learning models often need to adapt to new data after deployment due to structured or unstructured real-world dynamics. The Continual Learning (CL) framework enables continuous model adaptation, but most existing approaches either assume each task contains sufficiently many data samples or that the learning tasks are non-overlapping. In this paper, we address the more general setting where each task may have a limited dataset, and tasks may overlap in an arbitrary manner without a priori knowledge. This general setting is substantially more challenging for two reasons. On the one hand, data scarcity necessitates effective contextualization of general knowledge and efficient knowledge transfer across tasks. On the other hand, unstructured task overlapping can easily result in negative knowledge transfer. To address the above challenges, we propose an adaptive mixture-of-experts (MoE) framework over pre-trained models that progressively establishes similarity awareness among tasks. Our design contains two innovative algorithmic components: incremental global pooling and instance-wise prompt masking. The former mitigates prompt association noise through gradual prompt introduction over time. The latter decomposes incoming task samples into those aligning with current prompts (in-distribution) and those requiring new prompts (out-of-distribution). Together, our design strategically leverages potential task overlaps while actively preventing negative mutual interference in the presence of per-task data scarcity. Experiments across varying data volumes and inter-task similarity show that our method enhances sample efficiency and is broadly applicable.

</details>


### [92] [End-to-End Efficient RL for Linear Bellman Complete MDPs with Deterministic Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23461)
*Zakaria Mhammedi,Alexander Rakhlin,Nneka Okolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种计算高效的强化学习算法，适用于具有线性Bellman完备性、确定性转移、随机初始状态和奖励的MDPs，支持有限和大动作空间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性Bellman完备MDPs的高效算法要么限于小动作空间，要么需要特征空间的强假设，需要开发更通用的高效算法

Method: 针对确定性转移、随机初始状态和奖励的线性Bellman完备MDPs，设计计算高效算法，有限动作空间时端到端高效，大/无限动作空间时仅需标准argmax oracle

Result: 算法能以多项式样本和计算复杂度学习ε-最优策略，复杂度在horizon、特征维度和1/ε上为多项式

Conclusion: 成功开发了线性Bellman完备MDPs的计算高效算法，解决了先前算法在动作空间规模和假设要求方面的限制

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning (RL) with linear function approximation in Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) satisfying \emph{linear Bellman completeness} -- a fundamental setting where the Bellman backup of any linear value function remains linear. While statistically tractable, prior computationally efficient algorithms are either limited to small action spaces or require strong oracle assumptions over the feature space. We provide a computationally efficient algorithm for linear Bellman complete MDPs with \emph{deterministic transitions}, stochastic initial states, and stochastic rewards. For finite action spaces, our algorithm is end-to-end efficient; for large or infinite action spaces, we require only a standard argmax oracle over actions. Our algorithm learns an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy with sample and computational complexity polynomial in the horizon, feature dimension, and $1/\varepsilon$.

</details>

### [93] [Why Database Manuals Are Not Enough: Efficient and Reliable Configuration Tuning for DBMSs via Code-Driven LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22708)
*Xinyi Zhang,Tiantian Chen,Zhentao Han,Zhaoyan Hong,Wei Lu,Sheng Wang,Mo Sha,Anni Wang,Shuang Liu,Yakun Zhang,Feifei Li,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SysInsight：一种基于代码分析的数据库自动调优系统，通过分析DBMS源代码提取细粒度调优知识，加速并稳定调优过程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库自动调优方法存在局限性：数据驱动方法开销大，基于文档的手动启发式方法有限且过于通用。由于配置旋钮的控制逻辑本质编码在DBMS源代码中，因此可以从代码中挖掘有前景的调优策略

Method: 结合静态代码分析与基于LLM的推理，识别旋钮控制的执行路径并提取语义调优洞察；通过基于调优观察的关联规则挖掘，将这些洞察转化为可量化、可验证的调优规则；在线调优时应用系统诊断识别关键旋钮，在规则指导下进行调整

Result: 相比最先进的基线方法，SysInsight平均以7.11倍的速度收敛到最佳配置，同时实现19.9%的性能提升

Conclusion: SysInsight通过从DBMS源代码中挖掘调优知识，提供了一种有效的数据驱动调优方法，显著加速调优过程并提升性能稳定性

Abstract: Modern database management systems (DBMSs) expose hundreds of configuration knobs that critically influence performance. Existing automated tuning methods either adopt a data-driven paradigm, which incurs substantial overhead, or rely on manual-driven heuristics extracted from database documentation, which are often limited and overly generic. Motivated by the fact that the control logic of configuration knobs is inherently encoded in the DBMS source code, we argue that promising tuning strategies can be mined directly from the code, uncovering fine-grained insights grounded in system internals. To this end, we propose SysInsight, a code-driven database tuning system that automatically extracts fine-grained tuning knowledge from DBMS source code to accelerate and stabilize the tuning process. SysInsight combines static code analysis with LLM-based reasoning to identify knob-controlled execution paths and extract semantic tuning insights. These insights are then transformed into quantitative and verifiable tuning rules via association rule mining grounded in tuning observations. During online tuning, system diagnosis is applied to identify critical knobs, which are adjusted under the rule guidance. Evaluations demonstrate that compared to the SOTA baseline, SysInsight converges to the best configuration on average 7.11X faster while achieving a 19.9% performance improvement.

</details>


### [94] [Spatial Analysis on Value-Based Quadtrees of Rasterized Vector Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23105)
*Diana Baumann,Nils Japke,Tim C. Rese,David Bermbach*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出基于值四叉树索引的向量-栅格数据联合空间分析方法，利用空间自相关特性，将点面查询延迟降低90%同时保持精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动数据科学需要同时处理向量和栅格数据，但现有空间分析工具往往偏向其中一种格式，缺乏对两者联合分析的支持，限制了数据科学家的工作效率

Method: 开发基于值的四叉树索引作为桥梁，利用向量和栅格数据的空间自相关特性，支持向量和栅格数据的联合空间分析

Result: 实现了90%的点面查询延迟中位数降低，同时保持查询响应的准确性不变

Conclusion: 基于值四叉树索引有效解决了向量和栅格数据联合分析的挑战，显著提升了空间查询性能，为移动数据科学提供了更高效的分析工具

Abstract: Mobility data science offers insights into the complex interconnections of spatial data of moving objects and their surroundings, often based on a combination of vector and raster data. For example, mobility traces are usually in vector format, weather data are often in raster format. Yet, available spatial analysis tools for exploratory data science push data scientists towards one or the other, providing only limited support for the respective other.
  In this paper, we contribute to this problem space with a value-based quadtree index, which serves as a bridge builder to support joint spatial analysis on vector and raster data leveraging their unique autocorrelation property. We achieve a 90% reduction in median Point-in-Polygon query latency, while keeping the accuracy of query responses at equal level.

</details>


### [95] [Natural Language Interfaces for Spatial and Temporal Databases: A Comprehensive Overview of Methods, Taxonomy, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23375)
*Samya Acharja,Kanchan Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文是关于地理空间和时空数据库自然语言接口(NLIDB)的综合性综述，分析了现有研究碎片化问题，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着地理空间数据集的激增，地理空间数据库在应用中扮演重要角色，但查询这些数据库与传统关系数据库存在显著差异，需要专门的地理空间拓扑算子和时间算子。现有NLIDB研究主要关注通用数据库系统，缺乏对地理空间和时空数据库的专门分析，且现有研究碎片化严重，难以清晰了解该领域现状。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，对地理空间和时空NLIDB研究进行全面调查，包括：数据集概述、评估指标分析、方法分类学构建，以及现有方法的比较分析。

Result: 调查揭示了现有方法的重复趋势、数据集和评估实践的显著差异，以及阻碍该领域进展的若干开放挑战。具体包括：方法创新有限、评估标准不统一、数据集多样性不足等问题。

Conclusion: 基于调查发现，论文识别了推进地理空间和时空数据库自然语言接口研究的几个有前景方向，包括：改进方法创新、统一评估标准、开发更丰富的数据集等，为该领域的未来发展提供了指导。

Abstract: The task of building a natural language interface to a database, known as NLIDB, has recently gained significant attention from both the database and Natural Language Processing (NLP) communities. With the proliferation of geospatial datasets driven by the rapid emergence of location-aware sensors, geospatial databases play a vital role in supporting geospatial applications. However, querying geospatial and temporal databases differs substantially from querying traditional relational databases due to the presence of geospatial topological operators and temporal operators. To bridge the gap between geospatial query languages and non-expert users, the geospatial research community has increasingly focused on developing NLIDBs for geospatial databases. Yet, existing research remains fragmented across systems, datasets, and methodological choices, making it difficult to clearly understand the landscape of existing methods, their strengths and weaknesses, and opportunities for future research. Existing surveys on NLIDBs focus on general-purpose database systems and do not treat geospatial and temporal databases as primary focus for analysis. To address this gap, this paper presents a comprehensive survey of studies on NLIDBs for geospatial and temporal databases. Specifically, we provide a detailed overview of datasets, evaluation metrics, and the taxonomy of the methods for geospatial and temporal NLIDBs, as well as a comparative analysis of the existing methods. Our survey reveals recurring trends in existing methods, substantial variation in datasets and evaluation practices, and several open challenges that continue to hinder progress in this area. Based on these findings, we identify promising directions for future research to advance natural language interfaces to geospatial and temporal databases.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [T-MAP: Red-Teaming LLM Agents with Trajectory-aware Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22341)
*Hyomin Lee,Sangwoo Park,Yumin Choi,Sohyun An,Seanie Lee,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: T-MAP：一种基于轨迹感知的进化搜索方法，用于发现LLM智能体在多步工具执行中的对抗性漏洞，特别针对Model Context Protocol生态系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队测试主要关注从大语言模型中引出有害文本输出，但未能捕捉到智能体特有的漏洞，这些漏洞在多步工具执行过程中出现，特别是在快速发展的Model Context Protocol生态系统中

Method: 提出T-MAP（轨迹感知进化搜索方法），利用执行轨迹来指导对抗性提示的发现，能够自动生成绕过安全防护并通过实际工具交互实现有害目标的攻击

Result: 在多种MCP环境中的实证评估表明，T-MAP在攻击实现率上显著优于基线方法，并且对前沿模型（包括GPT-5.2、Gemini-3-Pro、Qwen3.5和GLM-5）仍然有效，揭示了自主LLM智能体中先前未被充分探索的漏洞

Conclusion: T-MAP方法能够有效发现LLM智能体在多步工具执行中的对抗性漏洞，特别是在MCP生态系统中，为智能体安全评估提供了新的重要视角

Abstract: While prior red-teaming efforts have focused on eliciting harmful text outputs from large language models (LLMs), such approaches fail to capture agent-specific vulnerabilities that emerge through multi-step tool execution, particularly in rapidly growing ecosystems such as the Model Context Protocol (MCP). To address this gap, we propose a trajectory-aware evolutionary search method, T-MAP, which leverages execution trajectories to guide the discovery of adversarial prompts. Our approach enables the automatic generation of attacks that not only bypass safety guardrails but also reliably realize harmful objectives through actual tool interactions. Empirical evaluations across diverse MCP environments demonstrate that T-MAP substantially outperforms baselines in attack realization rate (ARR) and remains effective against frontier models, including GPT-5.2, Gemini-3-Pro, Qwen3.5, and GLM-5, thereby revealing previously underexplored vulnerabilities in autonomous LLM agents.

</details>


### [97] [BlindMarket: Enabling Verifiable, Confidential, and Traceable IP Core Distribution in Zero-Trust Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22685)
*Zhaoxiang Liu,Samuel Judson,Raj Dutta,Mark Santolucito,Xiaolong Guo,Ning Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BlindMarket是一个端到端的零信任硬件IP核分发框架，支持IP用户和供应商在交易前完成具有可验证性和保密性的IP交易，并在交易后提供可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决硬件IP核交易中的信任问题，提供交易前的可验证性和保密性保证，以及交易后的可追溯性，克服密码协议可扩展性有限和硬件验证困难等挑战。

Method: 提出验证启发式方法，采用基于影响锥的设计剪枝技术，结合控制流引导的启发式策略，构建端到端的零信任分发框架。

Result: 在13个真实硬件IP核设计中，成功验证了12个设计，通过设计剪枝和控制流引导启发式实现了显著的性能改进。

Conclusion: BlindMarket框架能够有效处理多样化的真实硬件IP核，在保证安全性的同时克服了传统方法的可扩展性限制，为硬件IP交易提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We present BlindMarket, an end-to-end zero-trust distribution framework for hardware IP cores. BlindMarket allows two parties, the IP user and the IP vendor, to complete an IP trading process with strong guarantees of verifiability and confidentiality before the transaction, and then traceability after. We propose verification heuristics and adapt the cone of influence-based design pruning to overcome the limited scalability common to cryptographic protocols and the hardness of the underlying hardware verification. We systematically evaluate our framework on a diverse set of real-world hardware benchmarks, and the results demonstrate that BlindMarket effectively completes across a diverse set of real-world hardware IP cores, demonstrating successful verification on 12 out of 13 designs and substantial performance improvements enabled by design pruning and control-flow guided heuristics.

</details>


### [98] [mmFHE: mmWave Sensing with End-to-End Fully Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22437)
*Tanvir Ahmed,Yixuan Gao,Adnan Armouti,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: mmFHE是首个实现毫米波雷达端到端全同态加密的系统，在边缘设备加密原始距离剖面数据，在不可信云上同态执行完整的毫米波信号处理和机器学习推理流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达传感应用日益普及，但原始传感数据包含大量隐私信息，传统加密方法无法在加密状态下进行计算处理。需要一种既能保护数据隐私，又能在云上进行完整信号处理和推理的解决方案。

Method: 开发了七个可组合的数据不可知FHE内核库，这些内核用固定算术电路替代标准DSP例程，可灵活组合成不同应用特定流水线。系统在边缘设备加密原始距离剖面数据，在云上同态执行整个处理流程。

Result: 在三个公开雷达数据集（270个生命体征记录，600个手势试验）上评估显示，加密引入的误差可忽略：HR/RR MAE <10^-3 bpm（与明文相比），手势识别准确率84.5%（明文为84.7%）。端到端云GPU延迟：10秒生命体征窗口103秒，3秒手势窗口37秒。

Conclusion: mmFHE证明了在商用硬件上实现隐私保护的端到端毫米波传感是可行的，系统提供输入隐私和数据不可知性两个密码学保证，有效抵御各种监督和非监督隐私攻击。

Abstract: We present mmFHE, the first system that enables fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) for end-to-end mmWave radar sensing. mmFHE encrypts raw range profiles on a lightweight edge device and executes the entire mmWave signal-processing and ML inference pipeline homomorphically on an untrusted cloud that operates exclusively on ciphertexts. At the core of mmFHE is a library of seven composable, data-oblivious FHE kernels that replace standard DSP routines with fixed arithmetic circuits. These kernels can be flexibly composed into different application-specific pipelines. We demonstrate this approach on two representative tasks: vital-sign monitoring and gesture recognition. We formally prove two cryptographic guarantees for any pipeline assembled from this library: input privacy, the cloud learns nothing about the sensor data; and data obliviousness, the execution trace is identical on the cloud regardless of the data being processed. These guarantees effectively neutralize various supervised and unsupervised privacy attacks on raw data, including re-identification and data-dependent privacy leakage. Evaluation on three public radar datasets (270 vital-sign recordings, 600 gesture trials) shows that encryption introduces negligible error: HR/RR MAE <10^-3 bpm versus plaintext, and 84.5% gesture accuracy (vs. 84.7% plaintext) with end-to-end cloud GPU latency of 103s for a 10s vital-sign window and 37s for a 3s gesture window. These results show that privacy-preserving end-to-end mmWave sensing is feasible on commodity hardware today.

</details>


### [99] [Privacy-Aware Smart Cameras: View Coverage via Socially Responsible Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23197)
*Chuhao Qin,Lukas Esterle,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化智能摄像头协调框架，通过集体学习自主选择朝向，在监控公共空间的同时通过软硬约束排除隐私敏感区域，实现大规模覆盖效率与隐私保护的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有摄像头系统要么过度追求覆盖范围而忽视隐私，要么过度依赖昂贵的加密技术。需要一种更负责任的城市智能方案，让摄像头能够协调监控公共空间，同时通过设计排除隐私敏感区域。

Method: 提出去中心化框架，让交互式智能摄像头通过集体学习自主选择朝向，采用软约束和硬约束满足来消除隐私违规。无需集中控制即可扩展到数百至数千个摄像头。

Result: 实验结果显示，与基线和其他最先进方法相比，覆盖效率提高18.42%，隐私违规降低85.53%。该框架为大规模敏感城市环境中的摄像头部署提供了实用指南。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了通过伦理对齐的人工智能协调智能摄像头，能够在保持去中心化扩展性的同时，显著提高公共空间覆盖效率并大幅减少隐私违规，为城市智能监控提供了更负责任的技术方案。

Abstract: Coordination of view coverage via privacy-aware smart cameras is key to a more socially responsible urban intelligence. Rather than maximizing view coverage at any cost or over relying on expensive cryptographic techniques, we address how cameras can coordinate to legitimately monitor public spaces while excluding privacy-sensitive regions by design. This article proposes a decentralized framework in which interactive smart cameras coordinate to autonomously select their orientation via collective learning, while eliminating privacy violations via soft and hard constraint satisfaction. The approach scales to hundreds up to thousands of cameras without any centralized control. Experimental evidence shows 18.42% higher coverage efficiency and 85.53% lower privacy violation than baselines and other state-of-the-art approaches. This significant advance further unravels practical guidelines for operators and policymakers: how the field of view, spatial placement, and budget of cameras operating by ethically-aligned artificial intelligence jointly influence coverage efficiency and privacy protection in large-scale and sensitive urban environments.

</details>


### [100] [Architecture-Derived CBOMs for Cryptographic Migration: A Security-Aware Architecture Tradeoff Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22442)
*Eduard Hirsch,Kristina Raab*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SATAM方法通过安全感知架构评估生成架构驱动的CBOM，为密码迁移提供上下文信息


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CBOM通常基于工具或清单，缺乏架构意图、原理和安全上下文，限制了其在密码迁移规划中的实用性

Method: 提出SATAM方法，整合ATAM、arc42、STRIDE、ADR和CARAF等方法，识别分析安全相关的密码决策点，并将其记录为明确的架构决策，使用CycloneDX兼容扩展为CBOM条目添加架构上下文

Result: 架构驱动的CBOM能够捕获迁移相关的上下文信息，这些信息在基于清单的方法中通常缺失，从而改善密码迁移规划所需信息的可用性

Conclusion: SATAM通过提供架构驱动的、上下文敏感的CBOM，增强了密码迁移规划的信息可用性和长期密码敏捷性

Abstract: Cryptographic migration driven by algorithm deprecation, regulatory change, and post-quantum readiness requires more than an inventory of cryptographic assets. Existing Cryptographic Bills of Materials (CBOMs) are typically tool- or inventory-derived. They lack architectural intent, rationale, and security context, limiting their usefulness for migration planning. This paper introduces Security-Aware Architecture Tradeoff Analysis Method (SATAM), a security-aware adaptation of scenario-based architecture evaluation that derives an architecture-grounded, context-sensitive CBOM. SATAM integrates established approaches: ATAM, arc42, STRIDE, ADR, and CARAF. These are included to identify and analyze security-relevant cryptographic decision points and document them as explicit architectural decisions. These artifacts are used to annotate CBOM entries with architectural context, security intent, and migration-critical metadata using CycloneDX-compatible extensions. Following a Design Science Research approach, the paper presents the method design, a conceptual traceability model, and an illustrative application. The results demonstrate that architecture-derived CBOMs capture migration-relevant context that is typically absent from inventory-based approaches. Thereby, SATAM improves availability of information required for informed cryptographic migration planning and long-term cryptographic agility.

</details>


### [101] [Does Teaming-Up LLMs Improve Secure Code Generation? A Comprehensive Evaluation with Multi-LLMSecCodeEval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22717)
*Bushra Sabir,Shigang Liu,Seung Ick Jang,Sharif Abuadbba,Yansong Gao,Kristen Moore,SangCheol Kim,Hyoungshick Kim,Surya Nepal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 多LLM集成与协作策略能显著提升安全代码生成，集成静态分析的集成管道比单LLM基线提升47.3%，混合管道（集成、检测、修复）实现最强安全性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在自然语言生成源代码方面取得进展，但安全漏洞问题仍然存在。本研究旨在系统评估多LLM集成和协作策略是否能显著改善安全代码生成

Method: 提出MULTI-LLMSECCODEEVAL框架，结合多个LLM、静态分析和结构化协作来评估和增强安全漏洞管理生命周期。使用SecLLMEval和SecLLMHolmes基准测试十个管道，涵盖单模型、集成、协作和混合设计

Result: 集成静态分析的集成管道在SecLLMEval上比单LLM基线提升47.3%，在SecLLMHolmes上提升19.3%。纯LLM协作管道提升较小（8.9%-22.3%）。混合管道（集成、检测、修复）表现最佳，比最佳集成基线提升1.78%-4.72%，比协作基线提升19.81%-26.78%

Conclusion: 安全代码生成不依赖于模型规模，而是需要精心设计的多模型系统架构。小型结构化多模型集成持续优于大型单体LLM，证明安全代码来自精心编排的多模型系统设计而非规模

Abstract: Automatically generating source code from natural language using large language models (LLMs) is becoming common, yet security vulnerabilities persist despite advances in fine tuning and prompting. In this work, we systematically evaluate whether multi LLM ensembles and collaborative strategies can meaningfully improve secure code generation. We present MULTI-LLMSECCODEEVAL, a framework for assessing and enhancing security across the vulnerability management lifecycle by combining multiple LLMs with static analysis and structured collaboration. Using SecLLMEval and SecLLMHolmes, we benchmark ten pipelines spanning single model, ensemble, collaborative, and hybrid designs. Our results show that ensemble pipelines augmented with static analysis improve secure code generation over single LLM baselines by up to 47.3% on SecLLMEval and 19.3% on SecLLMHolmes, while purely LLM based collaborative pipelines yield smaller gains of 8.9% to 22.3%. Hybrid pipelines that integrate ensembling, detection, and patching achieve the strongest security performance, outperforming the best ensemble baseline by 1.78% to 4.72% and collaborative baselines by 19.81% to 26.78%. Ablation studies reveal that model scale alone does not ensure security. Smaller, structured multi model ensembles consistently outperform large monolithic LLMs. Overall, our findings demonstrate that secure code does not emerge from scale, but from carefully orchestrated multi model system design.

</details>


### [102] [CIPL: A Target-Independent Framework for Channel-Inversion Privacy Leakage in Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22751)
*Tao Huang,Chen Hou,Jiayang Meng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CIPL框架将智能体系统中的隐私泄露建模为信道反转问题，提供跨系统攻击的统一分析框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注内存泄露，但攻击方法局限于特定系统和输出表面，缺乏统一的分析框架来研究智能体系统中敏感信息通过内部处理流程泄露的风险

Method: 提出CIPL（Channel Inversion for Privacy Leakage）框架，将目标系统建模为包含敏感源、选择、组装、执行、观察和提取阶段的通用签名，攻击通过定位器、对齐器和多样化策略的可重用攻击语言实例化

Result: 隐私泄露不仅限于内存，而是取决于敏感内容如何路由到攻击者可见的观察信道，CIPL支持跨目标比较同时保留目标特定的执行语义

Conclusion: 智能体系统的隐私评估应从单一表面攻击分析转向面向信道的信息暴露视角，CIPL为研究此类攻击提供了统一框架

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents may expose sensitive information through more than their final textual responses. Whenever private content is internally selected, assembled, and reused inside an agent pipeline, an attacker may attempt to turn that hidden dependence into an observable output signal. Existing evidence of this risk is strongest for memory leakage, but current attack formulations remain largely tied to specific systems and output surfaces. In this paper, we formulate privacy leakage in agentic systems as a \emph{channel inversion} problem and present CIPL (Channel Inversion for Privacy Leakage), a target-independent framework for studying such attacks. CIPL represents a target system through a common signature consisting of a sensitive source, selection, assembly, execution, observation, and extraction stages, and instantiates attacks through a reusable attack language built from a locator, an aligner, and a diversification policy. As a unified evaluation framework, CIPL supports cross-target comparison while preserving target-specific execution semantics. Our results provide initial evidence that privacy leakage is not confined to memory alone; instead, it depends on how sensitive content is routed into attacker-visible observation channels. These findings suggest that privacy evaluation for agentic systems should move beyond single-surface attack analysis toward a channel-oriented view of information exposure.

</details>


### [103] [Combinatorial Privacy: Private Multi-Party Bitstream Grand Sum by Hiding in Birkhoff Polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22808)
*Praneeth Vepakomma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PolyVeil协议使用Birkhoff多面体中的置换矩阵编码私有比特，实现k个客户端间的私有布尔求和，具有完美模拟安全性，聚合器面临#P-hard推理问题，在隐私保护与计算复杂度间存在根本性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一种无需PKI、通信开销为O(k)的私有布尔求和协议，在保证服务器端完美模拟安全性的同时，使聚合器面临计算上不可行的推理问题，从而保护客户端隐私。

Method: 采用两层架构：1) 客户端将私有比特编码为Birkhoff多面体中的置换矩阵；2) 服务器获得完美模拟安全性；3) 聚合器面临基于永久函数和混合判别式的#P-hard似然推理。提供完整变体（聚合器观察双随机矩阵）和压缩变体（聚合器观察单个标量）。

Result: 1) 完整变体中log-Lipschitz常数以n^4 K_t增长，DP保证仅在私有信号不可检测时非平凡；2) 压缩变体在中等信噪比下可实现非平凡的ε-DP；3) 揭示了#P-hardness需要完整矩阵视图，而非平凡DP需要标量视图的根本矛盾；4) 协议无需PKI，通信开销O(k)，输出精确聚合结果。

Conclusion: PolyVeil协议在隐私保护与计算复杂度间存在根本性权衡：#P-hardness需要完整的Birkhoff结构可见性，而非平凡DP保证需要低维标量视图。两种特性能否在同一变体中同时实现仍是开放问题。协议在实用性和安全性方面提供了有意义的折衷。

Abstract: We introduce PolyVeil, a protocol for private Boolean summation across $k$ clients that encodes private bits as permutation matrices in the Birkhoff polytope. A two-layer architecture gives the server perfect simulation-based security (statistical distance zero) while a separate aggregator faces \#P-hard likelihood inference via the permanent and mixed discriminant. Two variants (full and compressed) differ in what the aggregator observes.
  We develop a finite-sample $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP analysis with explicit constants. In the full variant, where the aggregator sees a doubly stochastic matrix per client, the log-Lipschitz constant grows as $n^4 K_t$ and a signal-to-noise analysis shows the DP guarantee is non-vacuous only when the private signal is undetectable. In the compressed variant, where the aggregator sees a single scalar, the univariate density ratio yields non-vacuous $\varepsilon$ at moderate SNR, with the optimal decoy count balancing CLT accuracy against noise concentration.
  This exposes a fundamental tension. \#P-hardness requires the full matrix view (Birkhoff structure visible), while non-vacuous DP requires the scalar view (low dimensionality). Whether both hold simultaneously in one variant remains open. The protocol needs no PKI, has $O(k)$ communication, and outputs exact aggregates.

</details>


### [104] [SoK: The Attack Surface of Agentic AI -- Tools, and Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22928)
*Ali Dehghantanha,Sajad Homayoun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统化分析了基于大语言模型的智能体系统安全风险，建立了攻击分类体系，评估了防御措施，并提出了安全部署指南和研究挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统从单纯的语言模型发展为结合工具、外部知识检索和自主决策的智能体范式，其能力扩展的同时也大幅增加了攻击面。当前缺乏对智能体LLM系统安全风险的系统性梳理，需要建立全面的威胁分类和防御框架。

Method: 通过文献综述方法，综合分析了2023-2025年间超过20项同行评审研究、行业报告和标准。开发了全面的攻击分类学，涵盖提示注入、知识库污染、工具/插件利用和多智能体威胁。定义了攻击者模型和威胁场景，提出了安全评估指标，并分析了现有防御措施的有效性。

Result: 研究发现智能体系统引入了传统AI威胁之外的新攻击向量，包括间接提示注入、代码执行利用、RAG索引污染和跨智能体操纵。现有防御措施如输入净化、检索过滤器、沙箱、访问控制和AI护栏等在某些方面有效，但在多个领域仍存在保护不足。提出了防御控制措施和分阶段安全清单。

Conclusion: 智能体AI系统面临独特且复杂的安全挑战，需要系统化的安全方法。本文为研究人员和工程师提供了理解与缓解这些风险的综合框架，并指出了安全自主AI领域的开放研究挑战，包括鲁棒工具API、可验证的智能体行为和供应链保障等方向。

Abstract: Recent AI systems combine large language models with tools, external knowledge via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and even autonomous multi-agent decision loops. This agentic AI paradigm greatly expands capabilities - but also vastly enlarges the attack surface. In this systematization, we map out the trust boundaries and security risks of agentic LLM-based systems. We develop a comprehensive taxonomy of attacks spanning prompt-level injections, knowledge-base poisoning, tool/plug-in exploits, and multi-agent emergent threats. Through a detailed literature review, we synthesize evidence from 2023-2025, including more than 20 peer-reviewed and archival studies, industry reports, and standards. We find that agentic systems introduce new vectors for indirect prompt injection, code execution exploits, RAG index poisoning, and cross-agent manipulation that go beyond traditional AI threats. We define attacker models and threat scenarios, and propose metrics (e.g., Unsafe Action Rate, Privilege Escalation Distance) to evaluate security posture. Our survey examines defenses such as input sanitization, retrieval filters, sandboxes, access control, and "AI guardrails," assessing their effectiveness and pointing out the areas where protection is still lacking. To assist practitioners, we outline defensive controls and provide a phased security checklist for deploying agentic AI (covering design-time hardening, runtime monitoring, and incident response). Finally, we outline open research challenges in secure autonomous AI (robust tool APIs, verifiable agent behavior, supply-chain safeguards) and discuss ethical and responsible disclosure practices. We systematize recent findings to help researchers and engineers understand and mitigate security risks in agentic AI.

</details>


### [105] [Beyond Theoretical Bounds: Empirical Privacy Loss Calibration for Text Rewriting Under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22968)
*Weijun Li,Arnaud Grivet Sébert,Qiongkai Xu,Annabelle McIver,Mark Dras*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了TeDA框架，用于在局部差分隐私下的文本重写机制之间进行经验校准，通过假设检验框架在表面和嵌入空间评估文本可区分性，揭示相似名义ε值可能对应不同的实际隐私保护水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的广泛应用，如何在隐私保护前提下共享文本数据成为重要挑战。现有基于局部差分隐私的文本重写方法使用ε参数来界定隐私损失上界，但名义ε值难以解释且在不同机制间缺乏可比性，需要经验校准方法来评估实际隐私保护效果。

Method: 提出TeDA框架，采用假设检验方法在表面空间和嵌入空间进行文本可区分性审计。该方法通过经验评估从隐私化文本中区分原始文本的能力，实现对不同LDP文本重写机制的实际隐私保护水平的校准和比较。

Result: 对多个代表性机制应用TeDA校准后发现，相似的名义ε界限可能对应完全不同的可区分性水平。经验校准为评估隐私-效用权衡提供了更可比较的基础，并为实际LDP文本重写部署中的机制比较和分析提供了实用工具。

Conclusion: 名义ε参数在评估局部差分隐私文本重写机制的实际隐私保护效果时存在局限性。TeDA框架通过经验校准方法提供了更准确、可比较的隐私评估，有助于在实际应用中更好地理解和选择适当的隐私-效用权衡。

Abstract: The growing use of large language models has increased interest in sharing textual data in a privacy-preserving manner. One prominent line of work addresses this challenge through text rewriting under Local Differential Privacy (LDP), where input texts are locally obfuscated before release with formal privacy guarantees. These guarantees are typically expressed by a parameter $\varepsilon$ that upper bounds the worst-case privacy loss. However, nominal $\varepsilon$ values are often difficult to interpret and compare across mechanisms. In this work, we investigate how to empirically calibrate across text rewriting mechanisms under LDP. We propose TeDA, which formulates calibration via a hypothesis-testing framework that instantiates text distinguishability audits in both surface and embedding spaces, enabling empirical assessment of indistinguishability from privatized texts. Applying this calibration to several representative mechanisms, we demonstrate that similar nominal $\varepsilon$ bounds can imply very different levels of distinguishability. Empirical calibration thus provides a more comparable footing for evaluating privacy-utility trade-offs, as well as a practical tool for mechanism comparison and analysis in real-world LDP text rewriting deployments.

</details>


### [106] [How Far Should We Need to Go : Evaluate Provenance-based Intrusion Detection Systems in Industrial Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22982)
*Yue Xiao,Ling Jiang,Sen Nie,Ding Li,Shi Wu,Ke Xu,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统评估了溯源入侵检测系统在工业场景中的性能，发现现有系统存在跨主机平台可移植性差、对真实攻击检测性能低、误报率高等问题，并提出了改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然溯源入侵检测系统在原始论文评估中表现良好，但其在工业场景中的实际性能尚不明确。论文旨在填补这一空白，通过系统评估分析PIDS在工业环境中的表现。

Method: 首先分析DARPA数据集与工业场景数据的差异，识别工业数据的三个新特征：异构多源输入、更强大的攻击者、日益复杂的良性活动。然后构建多个数据集，评估五种最先进的PIDS系统。

Result: 评估揭示了现有PIDS的挑战：跨不同主机和平台的可移植性差、对真实世界攻击的检测性能低、随着良性活动变化而误报率高。基于评估结果和工业实践，提出了降低误报的方法，可减少2/3的人工工作量。

Conclusion: 论文首次系统评估了PIDS在工业场景中的性能，揭示了现有系统的局限性，并提供了改进建议。提出的误报缓解方法显著减少了人工工作量，为未来PIDS研究提供了方向性指导。

Abstract: Provenance-based Intrusion Detection Systems (PIDSes) have been widely used to detect Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs). Although many studies achieve high performance in the evaluations of their original papers, their performance in industrial scenarios remains unclear. To fill this gap, we conduct the first systematic evaluation and analysis of PIDSes in industrial scenarios. We first analyze the differences between the data from DARPA datasets and that collected in industrial scenarios, identifying three main new characteristics in industry: heterogeneous multi-source inputs, more powerful attackers, and increasing benign activity complexity. We then build several datasets to evaluate five state-of-the-art PIDSes. The evaluation results reveal challenges for existing PIDSes, including poor portability across different hosts and platforms, low detection performance against real-world attacks, and high false positive rates with ever-changing benign activities. Based on the evaluation results and our industrial practices, we provide several insights to solve or explain the above problems. For example, we propose a method to mitigate the high false positives, which reduces manual effort by 2/3. Finally, we propose several research suggestions to improve PIDSes.

</details>


### [107] [A Critical Review on the Effectiveness and Privacy Threats of Membership Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22987)
*Najeeb Jebreel,David Sánchez,Josep Domingo-Ferrer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个评估框架来界定成员推理攻击何时构成真正的隐私威胁，并发现在实际条件下这些攻击仅代表弱隐私威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成员推理攻击已成为衡量机器学习隐私泄露的事实标准，但当前缺乏明确标准来判断这些攻击何时构成真正的隐私威胁，可能导致对风险的过度估计和不必要的模型效用牺牲。

Method: 提出一个评估框架，定义成员推理攻击构成真正隐私威胁的条件，并在此框架下评估代表性成员推理攻击。

Result: 在框架定义的实际条件下，成员推理攻击仅代表弱隐私威胁，表明将其作为隐私度量标准可能导致风险高估和防御过度。

Conclusion: 需要更精确的隐私威胁评估框架，避免因过度依赖成员推理攻击作为隐私度量而导致不必要的模型效用损失。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) aim to determine whether a data sample was included in a machine learning (ML) model's training set and have become the de facto standard for measuring privacy leakages in ML. We propose an evaluation framework that defines the conditions under which MIAs constitute a genuine privacy threat, and review representative MIAs against it. We find that, under the realistic conditions defined in our framework, MIAs represent weak privacy threats. Thus, relying on them as a privacy metric in ML can lead to an overestimation of risk and to unnecessary sacrifices in model utility as a consequence of employing too strong defenses.

</details>


### [108] [TRAP: Hijacking VLA CoT-Reasoning via Adversarial Patches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23117)
*Zhengxian Huang,Wenjun Zhu,Haoxuan Qiu,Xiaoyu Ji,Wenyuan Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于思维链推理的视觉语言动作模型存在安全漏洞，攻击者可通过对抗性补丁（如桌上的杯垫）劫持模型推理过程，导致机器人执行危险行为（如递送刀具而非苹果），而无需修改用户指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然思维链推理增强了视觉语言动作模型的泛化能力和可解释性，但其安全性尚未得到充分研究。本文旨在探索思维链推理机制是否存在新的攻击向量，特别是针对性的控制劫持攻击。

Method: 提出TRAP攻击框架：首先通过实证证明思维链推理对动作生成具有强支配性，即使与输入指令语义不一致；然后设计对抗性补丁（如杯垫）来破坏中间思维链推理；通过优化思维链对抗损失，诱导特定的攻击者定义行为。

Result: 在3种主流VLA架构和3种思维链推理范式上进行了广泛评估，验证了TRAP的有效性。攻击补丁通过打印在纸上在真实场景中实现，成功劫持了VLA模型的输出。

Conclusion: 思维链推理在VLA系统中引入了新的安全漏洞，攻击者可通过对抗性补丁劫持推理过程。研究结果强调了保护VLA系统中思维链推理安全的紧迫性。

Abstract: By integrating Chain-of-Thought(CoT) reasoning, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, particularly by improving generalization and interpretability. However, the security of CoT-based reasoning mechanisms remains largely unexplored.
  In this paper, we show that CoT reasoning introduces a novel attack vector for targeted control hijacking--for example, causing a robot to mistakenly deliver a knife to a person instead of an apple--without modifying the user's instruction. We first provide empirical evidence that CoT strongly governs action generation, even when it is semantically misaligned with the input instructions. Building on this observation, we propose TRAP, the first targeted adversarial attack framework for CoT-reasoning VLA models. TRAP uses an adversarial patch (e.g., a coaster placed on the table) to corrupt intermediate CoT reasoning and hijack the VLA's output. By optimizing the CoT adversarial loss, TRAP induces specific and adversary-defined behaviors. Extensive evaluations across 3 mainstream VLA architectures and 3 CoT reasoning paradigms validate the effectiveness of TRAP. Notably, we implemented the patch by printing it on paper in a real-world setting. Our findings highlight the urgent need to secure CoT reasoning in VLA systems.

</details>


### [109] [Robust Safety Monitoring of Language Models via Activation Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23171)
*Toluwani Aremu,Daniil Ognev,Samuele Poppi,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究大型语言模型(LLM)的安全监控问题，针对自适应攻击者设计了一种名为"激活水印"的防御机制，相比现有监控方法在对抗已知监控算法的攻击者时性能提升高达52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可能被滥用来泄露敏感信息（如武器制造指令或恶意软件编写）。LLM提供商依赖监控来检测和标记推理过程中的不安全行为。当前面临的安全挑战是自适应攻击者能够同时规避检测并引发不安全行为。由于LLM提供商无法修补其安全机制（因为他们不知道模型如何被滥用），自适应攻击者成为一个主要担忧。

Method: 将鲁棒的LLM监控建模为一个安全博弈：攻击者知道监控机制并试图提取敏感信息，而提供商必须以低误报率准确检测这些对抗性查询。提出"激活水印"防御方法，通过在推理过程中为攻击者精心引入不确定性来改进防御。

Result: 研究发现现有LLM监控对自适应攻击者存在脆弱性。激活水印方法在对抗知道监控算法但不知道密钥的攻击者时，性能优于基线防护方法高达52%。

Conclusion: 自适应攻击者对LLM安全构成严重威胁，而激活水印提供了一种有效的防御机制，通过在推理过程中引入不确定性来增强监控系统的鲁棒性，显著提升了对抗已知监控算法的攻击者的检测性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can be misused to reveal sensitive information, such as weapon-making instructions or writing malware. LLM providers rely on $\emph{monitoring}$ to detect and flag unsafe behavior during inference. An open security challenge is $\emph{adaptive}$ adversaries who craft attacks that simultaneously (i) evade detection while (ii) eliciting unsafe behavior. Adaptive attackers are a major concern as LLM providers cannot patch their security mechanisms, since they are unaware of how their models are being misused. We cast $\emph{robust}$ LLM monitoring as a security game, where adversaries who know about the monitor try to extract sensitive information, while a provider must accurately detect these adversarial queries at low false positive rates. Our work (i) shows that existing LLM monitors are vulnerable to adaptive attackers and (ii) designs improved defenses through $\emph{activation watermarking}$ by carefully introducing uncertainty for the attacker during inference. We find that $\emph{activation watermarking}$ outperforms guard baselines by up to $52\%$ under adaptive attackers who know the monitoring algorithm but not the secret key.

</details>


### [110] [PRETTINESS -- Privacy pResErving aTTrIbute maNagEment SyStem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23221)
*Jelizaveta Vakarjuk,Alisa Pankova*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个完整的端到端通用凭证撤销系统，基于单一服务器和安全传输通道，在通用可组合模型中证明安全性，并通过概念验证评估效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲数字身份钱包需要支持凭证撤销功能，虽然可以发行短期凭证，但在某些情况下不方便，需要独立的撤销服务允许随时撤销凭证

Method: 设计完整的端到端通用凭证撤销系统，技术基础是单一服务器和各方之间的安全传输通道，在通用可组合模型中形式化证明安全性

Result: 提出了一个完整的撤销系统方案，在通用可组合模型中证明了其安全性，并通过概念验证实现评估了系统效率

Conclusion: 为欧洲数字身份钱包提供了一个可行的凭证撤销解决方案，满足监管框架要求，具有安全性和实用性

Abstract: European Digital Identity (EUDI) Wallet aims to provide end users with a way to get attested credentials from issuers, and present them to different relying parties. An important property mentioned in the regulatory frameworks is the possibility to revoke a previously issued credential. While it is possible to issue a short-lived credential, in some cases it may be inconvenient, and a separate revocation service which allows to revoke a credential at any time may be necessary. In this work, we propose a full end-to-end description of a generic credential revocation system, which technically relies on a single server and secure transmission channels between parties. We prove security of the proposed revocation functionality in the universal composability model, and estimate its efficiency based on a proof-of-concept implementation.

</details>


### [111] [Gyokuro: Source-assisted Private Membership Testing using Trusted Execution Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23226)
*Yoshimichi Nakatsuka,Nicolas Dutly,Kari Kostiainen,Srdjan Capkun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Gyokuro是一个基于可信执行环境(TEE)的源辅助私有成员测试(SPMT)方案，通过证明服务器已足够进展将数据项添加到数据库，而非证明数据项存在于数据库中，实现了高效、可扩展且隐私保护的成员测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统私有成员测试(PMT)协议在效率、可扩展性和隐私保护方面存在挑战。源辅助PMT(SPMT)在证书透明性和供应链审计等应用中很重要，但设计一个既高效、可扩展又隐私保护的方法仍然困难。

Method: Gyokuro采用不同于传统成员测试方案的方法：利用可信执行环境(TEE)生成证明，证明服务器已足够进展将数据项添加到数据库，而非证明数据项存在于数据库中。借助现有监控服务，客户端可以推断没有数据项从数据库中被移除。

Result: Gyokuro实现了7毫秒的成员测试延迟和约1400请求/秒/核心的吞吐量。通信和计算需求与数据库大小无关，支持大规模数据库的成员测试。

Conclusion: Gyokuro通过TEE和进度证明方法，提供了强大的隐私保证和高效率，消除了复杂低效的保护机制需求，实现了可扩展的源辅助私有成员测试。

Abstract: Private Membership Testing (PMT) protocols enable clients to verify whether a certain data item is included in a database without revealing the item to the database operator or other external parties. This paper examines Source-assisted PMT (SPMT), in which clients leverage compact data source-provided information issued when the data item is first submitted to the database. SPMT is relevant in applications such as certificate transparency and supply-chain auditing; yet, designing an approach that is efficient, scalable, and privacy-preserving remains a challenge.
  This work presents Gyokuro, which takes a different approach to conventional membership testing schemes. Instead of requesting the server to produce a proof attesting that a certain data item exists in the database, we leverage Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) to produce proofs demonstrating that the server has made enough progress to add the data item to the database. With the help of existing monitoring services, clients can infer that no items have been removed from the database. This allows Gyokuro to provide strong privacy guaranties and achieve high efficiency, as a client's membership testing query does not include any information regarding their interests, and eliminates the need for complex and inefficient protection mechanisms. Additionally, this approach enables membership testing on large-scale databases, since the communication and computation required are independent of the database size. Our evaluations show practical feasibility, achieving 7 ms membership testing latency and throughput of around 1400 requests/sec/core.

</details>


### [112] [The Power of Power Codes: New Classes of Easy Instances for the Linear Equivalence Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23230)
*Michele Battagliola,Anna-Lena Horlemann,Abhinaba Mazumder,Rocco Mora,Paolo Santini,Michael Schaller,Violetta Weger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Schur积方法从置换等价问题(PEP)到线性等价问题(LEP)，利用幂码、Frobenius自同构和Hermitian hull识别了大量易解LEP实例，并改进了当单项矩阵系数在有限域乘法子群时的PEP归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性等价问题(LEP)和置换等价问题(PEP)作为后量子密码方案(如LESS)的安全基础，近年来重新受到关注。已有研究使用Schur积解决PEP并识别了许多易解实例，本文旨在将这一方法扩展到更一般的LEP问题。

Method: 1. 将Schur积方法从PEP推广到LEP；2. 利用幂码(power codes)这一更一般的概念；3. 结合Frobenius自同构和Hermitian hull；4. 当单项矩阵系数在有限域乘法子群时，提出改进的PEP归约方法。

Result: 1. 识别了大量易解LEP实例类别；2. 首次利用代数弱点解决LEP问题；3. 在特定条件下实现了更优的PEP归约；4. 扩展了Schur积方法在编码等价问题中的应用范围。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Schur积方法从PEP扩展到LEP，通过幂码、Frobenius自同构和Hermitian hull的组合，识别了众多易解LEP实例，为后量子密码方案的安全性分析提供了新的代数工具，并改进了特定条件下的PEP归约。

Abstract: Given two linear codes, the Linear Equivalence Problem (LEP) asks to find (if it exists) a linear isometry between them; as a special case, we have the Permutation Equivalence Problem (PEP), in which isometries must be permutations. LEP and PEP have recently gained renewed interest as the security foundations for several post-quantum schemes, including LESS. A recent paper has introduced the use of the Schur product to solve PEP, identifying many new easy-to-solve instances. In this paper, we extend this result to LEP. In particular, we generalize the approach and rely on the more general notion of power codes. Combining it with Frobenius automorphisms and Hermitian hulls, we identify many classes of easy LEP instances. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work exploiting algebraic weaknesses for LEP. Finally we show an improved reduction to PEP whenever the coefficients of the monomial matrix are in a subgroup of the multiplicative group of the finite field.

</details>


### [113] [On the Vulnerability of FHE Computation to Silent Data Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23253)
*Jianan Mu,Ge Yu,Zhaoxuan Kan,Song Bian,Liang Kong,Zizhen Liu,Cheng Liu,Jing Ye,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文对全同态加密计算中的软错误进行系统性评估，通过大规模故障注入实验和理论分析，揭示了FHE计算对瞬态硬件故障的脆弱性，并评估了不同容错机制的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着FHE技术逐渐走向实际部署，其在真实硬件上的计算可靠性问题尚未充分探索。FHE相比明文计算具有更高的计算开销，因此更容易受到瞬态硬件故障的影响，且由于服务无法访问底层明文，数据损坏可能保持静默，导致用户无法察觉结果已损坏。

Method: 采用大规模故障注入实验来表征FHE对瞬态故障的脆弱性，同时通过理论分析错误传播行为，深入了解这种脆弱性的算法机制，并评估不同容错机制对缓解这些故障的有效性。

Result: 研究发现FHE密文计算对软错误高度脆弱，通过实验和理论分析揭示了错误传播的具体机制，并评估了各种容错机制在缓解这些故障方面的效果。

Conclusion: FHE计算在真实硬件上存在显著的可靠性风险，需要专门的容错机制来确保其在实际部署中的可靠性，特别是在金融、生物医学分析和隐私保护AI等关键应用领域。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) is rapidly emerging as a promising foundation for privacy-preserving cloud services, enabling computation directly on encrypted data. As FHE implementations mature and begin moving toward practical deployment in domains such as secure finance, biomedical analytics, and privacy-preserving AI, a critical question remains insufficiently explored: how reliable is FHE computation on real hardware? This question is especially important because, compared with plaintext computation, FHE incurs much higher computational overhead, making it more susceptible to transient hardware faults. Moreover, data corruptions are likely to remain silent: the FHE service has no access to the underlying plaintext, causing unawareness even though the corresponding decrypted result has already been corrupted. To this end, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation of SDCs in FHE ciphertext computation. Through large-scale fault-injection experiments, we characterize the vulnerability of FHE to transient faults, and through a theoretical analysis of error-propagation behaviors, we gain deeper algorithmic insight into the mechanisms underlying this vulnerability. We further assess the effectiveness of different fault-tolerance mechanisms for mitigating these faults.

</details>


### [114] [Canonical Byte-String Encoding for Finite-Ring Cryptosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23364)
*Kyrylo Riabov,Serhii Kryvyi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出了base-m长度编解码器，这是一种从字节串到模m剩余列表的规范映射，用于环映射协议中的字节到剩余转换层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环映射协议在进行代数加密步骤之前需要一个规范的字节到剩余转换层。当前缺乏一个标准化的、高效的、可验证的解决方案来处理字节串到模m剩余列表的转换。

Method: 基于Duda提出的rANS系统，设计了base-m长度编解码器，包含固定宽度头部（携带字节长度）和有效载荷。提供了Rust实现、Lean 4形式化验证（包含机器检查证明）和性能基准测试。

Result: 编解码器对所有满足参数界限的模数实现精确解码，对附加的有效后缀数字具有容错性。Lean 4形式化验证证明了固定宽度前缀可逆性、有效载荷状态界限低于2^64、流级往返正确性，以及所有输出符号都是有效的模m剩余。

Conclusion: 该工作为环映射协议提供了一个经过形式化验证的、高效的字节到剩余转换层，解决了实际应用中的关键问题，并提供了完整的实现和验证框架。

Abstract: Ring-mapping protocols need a canonical byte-to-residue layer before any algebraic encryption step can begin. This paper isolates that layer and presents the base-m length codec, a canonical map from byte strings of length less than 2^64 to lists of residues modulo m. The encoder builds on and adapts an rANS-based system proposed by Duda. Decoding is exact for all moduli satisfying the paper's parameter bounds. Because the encoding carries the byte length in its fixed-width header, decoding is also tolerant to appended valid suffix digits. The paper is accompanied by a Rust implementation of the described protocol, a Lean 4 formalization of the abstract codec with machine-checked proofs, and performance benchmarks. The Lean 4 formalization establishes fixed-width prefix inversion and payload-state bounds below 2^64, stream-level roundtrip correctness, and that every emitted symbol is a valid residue modulo m.
  We conclude with a complexity analysis and a discussion of practical considerations arising in real-world use of the codec.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [SkillClone: Multi-Modal Clone Detection and Clone Propagation Analysis in the Agent Skill Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22447)
*Jiaying Zhu,Lyuye Zhang,Wenbo Guo,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SkillClone：首个多模态智能体技能克隆检测方法，通过融合YAML、自然语言和代码三个通道的证据，有效识别技能间的克隆关系，在基准测试中达到F1分数0.939，应用于20K技能发现75%技能存在克隆关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前存在196K公开可用的智能体技能，但缺乏检测技能间克隆关系的机制，导致广泛复制的技能中的漏洞会无声地传播到衍生版本中，给维护者带来系统性风险。现有的单模态代码克隆检测器无法处理技能的多模态结构（YAML元数据、自然语言指令和嵌入式代码）。

Method: SkillClone采用多模态克隆检测方法，将平坦的TF-IDF相似度与按通道分解（YAML、自然语言、代码）通过逻辑回归融合，结合强大的检测能力和可解释的类型分类。构建了SkillClone-Bench基准，包含300个真实克隆对，按难度分层。

Result: 在SkillClone-Bench上，SkillClone达到F1分数0.939，精确率0.952，优于平坦TF-IDF（F1=0.881），在Type-4（语义）召回率上比MinHash高4.2倍。应用于20K技能发现258K克隆对，涉及75%的技能，40%跨越作者边界。去重分析显示生态系统膨胀了3.5倍，仅5,642个独特技能概念支撑着20K列出的技能，41%克隆家族中的技能已被严格更好的变体取代。

Conclusion: SkillClone是首个有效的多模态智能体技能克隆检测方法，揭示了智能体技能生态系统中广泛存在的克隆现象，为技能质量评估、漏洞追踪和生态系统优化提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Agent skills are modular instruction packages that combine YAML metadata, natural language instructions, and embedded code, and they have reached 196K publicly available instances, yet no mechanism exists to detect clone relationships among them. This gap creates systemic risks: a vulnerability in a widely copied skill silently persists across derivatives with no alert to maintainers. Existing clone detectors, designed for single-modality source code, cannot handle the multi-modal structure of skills, where clone evidence is distributed across three interleaved content channels. We present SkillClone, the first multi-modal clone detection approach for agent skills. SkillClone fuses flat TF-IDF similarity with per-channel decomposition (YAML, NL, code) through logistic regression, combining strong detection with interpretable type classification. We construct SkillClone-Bench, a balanced benchmark of 300 ground-truth pairs with stratified difficulty. On SkillClone-Bench, SkillClone achieves F1 of 0.939 with precision 0.952, outperforming flat TF-IDF (F1 = 0.881) and achieving 4.2x higher Type-4 (semantic) recall than MinHash. Applying SkillClone to 20K skills reveals 258K clone pairs involving 75% of all skills, with 40% crossing author boundaries. A deduplication analysis shows the ecosystem is inflated 3.5x: only 5,642 unique skill concepts underlie the 20K listed skills, and 41% of skills in clone families are superseded by a strictly better variant.

</details>


### [116] [LLMON: An LLM-native Markup Language to Leverage Structure and Semantics at the LLM Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22519)
*Michael Hind,Basel Shbita,Bo Wu,Farhan Ahmed,Chad DeLuca,Nathan Fulton,David Cox,Dan Gutfreund*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLMON是一种LLM原生标记语言，用于在文本中传达结构和语义元数据，提高模型准确性、安全性和安全性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM使用纯文本接口，无法区分指令和数据等结构化信息，导致模型混淆和安全风险（如提示注入攻击）

Method: 提出LLMON（LLM对象表示法）标记语言，设计满足LLM原生需求的语言规范，支持在模型训练、提示和推理实现中利用结构化信息

Result: LLMON能够自然地向LLM传达文本结构和语义元数据，为模型训练和推理实现提供初步实证证据，支持准确性、安全性和安全性改进

Conclusion: LLMON解决了LLM纯文本接口的局限性，类比编程语言类型系统的多种用途，为LLM原生方法开辟了新的研究机会
